### [1] [Distributions of Mesh Patterns of Short Lengths on Separable Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08865)
*Alice L. L. Gao,Sergey Kitaev,Ya-Xing Li,Yun Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将网格模式（mesh pattern）分布的研究拓展至可分排列（separable permutations），系统分析了长度至多为2的网格模式，确定其分布并借助计算机实验与对称性操作对相关等价类进行划分与枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格模式在排列中的分布是排列模式研究的重要方向，但已有工作多集中于一般排列，针对可分排列的系统分析尚不充分，因此作者希望将这一研究方向拓展到可分排列并给出完整的分布刻画。

Method: 作者针对可分排列，对长度至多为2的网格模式进行分类分析：对长度1的模式，确定全部六个等价类的分布，并求得含严格不动点统计量所在类的联合分布；对长度2的模式，通过计算机实验检验等分布性，并利用对称性操作将候选项划分为等价类，再通过显式推导分布（常为联合等分布）来合并等价类。

Result: 确定了长度1网格模式全部六个等价类的分布，以及严格不动点所在类的联合分布；发现类型12与21、着色相同的网格模式对中至多124对等分布，经对称性操作划分为38个分布等价类，显式确定其中24个分布后等价类数量降至31，并证明31即为真实等价类数目，剩余14个类的枚举留作公开问题。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了可分排列中长度至多为2的网格模式分布，通过对称性与显式枚举将分布等价类缩减至真实的31个，为网格模式在受限排列类中的研究提供了完整框架，并留下14个待枚举的开放问题。

Abstract: This paper contributes to the long line of research on the distribution of mesh patterns in permutations. We extend these studies to separable permutations and carry out a comprehensive analysis of mesh patterns of length at most~2. For mesh patterns of length~1, we determine the distributions for all six equivalence classes. In addition, we obtain the joint distribution for the patterns in the class containing the well-known permutation statistic known as the strict fixed point.
  Furthermore, computer experiments suggest that at most 124 pairs of mesh patterns of type 12 and 21 with identical shading are equidistributed. Using symmetry operations, we partition these pairs into 38 equivalence classes with respect to distribution. By explicitly determining 24 of these distributions (in many cases, in fact, joint equidistributions of the respective pairs), we reduce the number of equivalence classes to~31, which turns out to be the true number of equivalence classes. Enumerating 14 of these classes is left as an open problem.

</details>


### [2] [Spectral Extremal 1-Planar Graphs with Bounded Pentagon Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08888)
*Zhanhe Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 确定 1-平面图中不含 tC5 的最大邻接谱半径的 n 顶点图，并证明其唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Li、Wang 和 Zhao 提出的 Problem 1，即在 1-平面图类中刻画不含 tC5 的谱极值图。

Method: 先给出极值图的结构刻画：删除两个支配顶点后，剩余部分由 B=K1∨2K3 的副本和至多一个有界连通核组成；依赖于 1-平面 K2-join 的两族覆盖定理及紧的 packing-defect 不等式 12v(Q)−7e(Q)≥1−10ν5(Q)；再用归一化 resolvent 抵消重复 B-分量，通过有限矩比较迫使所有 packing 与非零 defect 落入一个核，并按模 7 的剩余类唯一识别该核；t=3 情形由精确有限分量引理完成。

Result: 对每个固定 t≥3 及充分大的 n，确定了唯一的 n 顶点 tC5-free 1-平面图具有最大邻接谱半径；同时得到有界五边形 packing 剩余部分的精确边极值结果。

Conclusion: 完整回答了 Li–Wang–Zhao 的问题，并建立了一个可用于谱极值与边极值问题的 packing-defect 框架。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal P_1$ denote the class of 1-planar graphs and let $tC_5$ be the disjoint union of $t$ copies of $C_5$. For every fixed $t\ge3$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the unique $n$-vertex $tC_5$-free graph in $\mathcal P_1$ with maximum adjacency spectral radius, answering Problem 1 of Li, Wang and Zhao. The proof first gives a structural description of every extremizer. After two dominating vertices are removed, the remainder consists of copies of the seven-vertex graph $B=K_1\vee2K_3$ together with at most one bounded connected core. This follows from a two-family covering theorem for 1-planar $K_2$-joins and the sharp packing--defect inequality \[ 12v(Q)-7e(Q)\ge1-10ν_5(Q). \] The same inequality yields an exact edge-extremal result for remainders with bounded pentagon packing. A normalized resolvent then cancels the repeated $B$-components, and finite moment comparisons force all packing and all nonzero defect into one core and identify that core uniquely in each residue class modulo $7$. The case $t=3$ is the pentagon-free boundary case and is completed by one exact finite component lemma.

</details>


### [3] [Positivity for graph Laurent phenomenon algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08914)
*Ross Glew*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了无自环、无重边的有向图所对应的Laurent现象代数中，任意簇变量关于任意簇种子展开的系数均非负，从而在该情形下解决了Lam–Pylyavskyy猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Laurent现象代数是簇代数的推广，Lam与Pylyavskyy在探讨有向图族的性质时，已证明每个簇变量关于任意簇的变量都是Laurent多项式，并进一步猜想这些展开具有非负系数。该猜想是否成立是理解其正性结构的关键问题。

Method: 针对无自环、无重边的有向图所关联的Laurent现象代数，在任意簇种子的框架下研究簇变量的Laurent展开，证明其系数非负。

Result: 对所有无自环、无重边的有向图，证明了簇变量关于任意簇种子的Laurent展开系数均为非负，即该猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了Lam–Pylyavskyy关于Laurent现象代数展开系数非负性的猜想在无自环、无重边有向图这一情形下成立，推广了簇代数中的正性结论。

Abstract: Laurent phenomenon algebras were introduced by Lam and Pylyavskyy as a generalisation of cluster algebras. For a family associated with directed graphs, they showed that every cluster variable is a Laurent polynomial in the variables of any cluster and conjectured that these expansions have nonnegative coefficients. We prove this conjecture for all directed graphs without loops or multiple edges with respect to an arbitrary seed cluster.

</details>


### [4] [Primitive periods in greedy chip-firing games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08949)
*Zheng Huang,Anyuan Tian*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究Li-Propp饥饿博弈及其推广模型的周期轨道结构。证明有限不可约有理转移矩阵下每个周期轨道的极小周期恰为T，状态i在周期内被点火Tπ_i次，且总饥饿为零的周期状态经格平移铺满相应超平面，由此解决Li-Propp的两个猜想；同时给出强连通有向图上固定优先级chip-firing的类似极小周期定理，并揭示饥饿博弈与chairman assignment问题的联系，进而推广到单调分数饥饿博弈，证明极小周期仍为T并给出最终周期性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Li和Propp提出的饥饿博弈是一种贪心chip-firing过程，其周期轨道结构与Li-Propp的两个猜想尚未被解决。此外，该贪心模型与chairman assignment等分配规则存在潜在联系，需要建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 针对有限不可约有理转移矩阵P，设π为平稳分布，T为使Tπ为整数的最小正整数，分析按最大饥饿点火（固定顺序破平）的贪婪过程。通过研究周期轨道沿格平移的几何结构证明极小周期为T。进一步将结论推广到强连通有向图上的固定优先级chip-firing，并定义非降分数函数的单调分数饥饿博弈，给出最终周期性的充分条件。

Result: 证明了每个周期轨道的极小周期为T，状态i恰好被点火Tπ_i次，总饥饿为零的周期状态集合通过格平移铺满对应超平面，解决Li-Propp两个猜想；对强连通有向图固定优先级chip-firing的非平凡周期轨道建立了类似极小周期定理；指出始终选择最低于比例目标状态的贪心规则即为秩一饥饿博弈；对单调分数饥饿博弈证明周期轨道极小周期仍为T并给出最终周期性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 饥饿博弈的周期结构由平稳分布π和最小整数化因子T完全刻画，该框架统一了chip-firing与chairman assignment等分配规则，并可通过单调分数推广保持极小周期定理，为贪心chip-firing的周期性分析提供了通用理论基础。

Abstract: For a finite irreducible rational transition matrix $P=(P_{ij})$ on $n$ states, the hunger game introduced by Li and Propp is a greedy chip-firing process in which each state $i$ carries a real-valued hunger $h_i$. At each step, a state $i$ of maximal hunger is selected, with ties resolved by a fixed order on the states. Firing $i$ subtracts one from its hunger and then adds $P_{ij}$ to the hunger of each state $j$. Let $π$ be the stationary distribution of $P$, and let $T$ be the least positive integer such that $Tπ$ is integral. We prove that every periodic orbit has least period $T$, with state $i$ firing $Tπ_i$ times, and that the set of periodic states of total hunger zero tiles the corresponding hyperplane by lattice translations. These results settle two conjectures of Li and Propp. We also prove an analogous least-period theorem for nontrivial periodic orbits of fixed-priority chip-firing on finite strongly connected digraphs, another greedy chip-firing model. We then find a connection between the hunger game and the chairman assignment problem: the greedy rule that always chooses the state furthest below its proportional target is precisely a rank-one hunger game. Motivated by this application and related allocation rules, we generalize the hunger game by assigning each state a score that is a nondecreasing function of its hunger and firing a state of maximal score each time. For these monotone-score hunger games, we prove that every periodic orbit still has least period $T$ and give a sufficient condition for eventual periodicity from every initial state.

</details>


### [5] [Distinguishing graphs with simple spectrum by homomorphism counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09042)
*Takanori Maehara*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究与图同态计数相关的图类如何决定具有简单谱的图，通过构造非同类同谱图给出必要条件，并刻画三阶子图同态计数的辨识能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨哪些图类能通过其成员到目标图的同态计数唯一决定（至同构）所有具有简单谱的图，从而刻画简单谱图的同态不变量。

Method: 将带根树附着到Cai-Fürer-Immerman图上构造非同类、同谱、二部、非奇异且最大度不超过6的图对，并证明其在每个minor-closed类上保持同态不可区分；随后研究三阶子图同态计数，证明其决定核维数至多为1的图，并给出等价刻画。

Result: 这样的图类必有无限树宽和无限Euler亏格，且包含以K7为minor的图；三阶子图同态计数决定所有核维数不超过1的图（推广非奇异图结果并适用于简单谱图）；两图三阶子图同态计数相同当且仅当顶点数相同且邻接矩阵像之间存在保持邻接算子与坐标三线性形式的双射线性等距；单顶点图与任意连通三次图序列的剖分图在完全图均为某成员minor时，其同态计数与三阶子图同态计数具有相同辨识力。

Conclusion: 简单谱图的同态辨识图类在结构上受显著限制，而三阶子图同态计数为核维数不超过1的图提供了完整的不变量刻画。

Abstract: We study which graph classes determine every graph with simple spectrum up to isomorphism by homomorphism counts from their members. We show that such a class has unbounded treewidth and Euler genus and contains a graph with the complete graph on seven vertices as a minor. To prove these conditions, we construct pairs of non-isomorphic cospectral graphs with simple spectrum, each bipartite, nonsingular and of maximum degree at most six. The construction attaches rooted trees to Cai-Furer-Immerman graphs and preserves homomorphism indistinguishability over every minor-closed class. We then prove that subcubic homomorphism counts determine every graph whose adjacency matrix has kernel dimension at most one. This extends the known result for nonsingular graphs and applies to all graphs with simple spectrum. We also prove that two graphs have the same subcubic homomorphism counts if and only if they have the same number of vertices and there is a bijective linear isometry between the images of their adjacency matrices that intertwines the adjacency operators and preserves the coordinatewise trilinear forms. Finally, we prove that homomorphism counts from the single-vertex graph and from subdivisions of any sequence of connected cubic graphs have the same distinguishing power as subcubic homomorphism counts if every complete graph is a minor of some member of the sequence.

</details>


### [6] [Inheritance of expansion and oriented Hamilton cycles in robust expanders: a regularity-free proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09072)
*Louis DeBiasio*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文重新审视Häggkvist–Thomason的证明，将其改造为不含正则引理的论证，从而给出Taylor定理（以及Kelly定理）的无正则引理证明，并进一步推广到Hamilton-连通与linkage问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kelly定理与Taylor定理的原有证明依赖Szemerédi正则引理，作者希望去除这一依赖，给出更直接的证明，并将其应用于最小半度至少n/2的有向图及最小总度至少(1+o(1))n的有向图中Hamilton圈任意定向的已有结果。

Method: 修改Häggkvist–Thomason的证明思路：先证明在线性最小半度的鲁棒外膨胀图中，两个均匀选取的对数大小不交集合以高概率满足Hall条件；再证明鲁棒膨胀性被均匀选取的线性大小集合继承；两处均使用Kleitman–Winston与Sapozhenko的graph-container方法，将可能的障碍归约到足够小的族以施加union bound。

Result: 得到Taylor定理与Kelly定理的无正则引理证明，并给出鲁棒外膨胀图中Hamilton-连通性与linkage相关结果的无正则引理证明；作为应用，替换了最小半度至少n/2的有向图以及最小总度至少(1+o(1))n的有向图中Hamilton圈任意定向相关已知结果里的正则引理使用。

Conclusion: Häggkvist–Thomason的原始方法经适当修改后可完全避开正则引理，足以证明Taylor的鲁棒外膨胀图定理及其若干推广与应用。

Abstract: Häggkvist and Thomason proved that every $n$-vertex oriented graph with minimum semidegree at least $(\frac{5}{12}+o(1))n$ contains every orientation of a Hamilton cycle. Using Szemerédi's regularity lemma, Kelly later improved this to the asymptotically sharp bound $(\frac{3}{8}+o(1))n$. Taylor subsequently generalized Kelly's theorem by proving that every sufficiently large robust outexpander with linear minimum semidegree contains every orientation of a Hamilton cycle. We revisit Häggkvist and Thomason's proof and show that it can be modified to give a regularity-free proof of Taylor's theorem (and thus Kelly's theorem). Building on this, we also give regularity-free proofs of related results on Hamilton-connectivity and linkage in robust outexpanders. As applications of these results, we are able to replace the use of the regularity lemma in known results on arbitrary orientations of Hamilton cycles in $n$-vertex digraphs with minimum semidegree at least $\frac{n}{2}$ and in $n$-vertex digraphs with minimum total degree at least $(1+o(1))n$.
  The first step is to show that, in an $n$-vertex robust outexpander with linear minimum semidegree, two uniformly chosen disjoint sets of logarithmic size satisfy Hall's condition with high probability. The second is to show that robust expansion is inherited by uniformly chosen linear sized sets. In both cases, we use the graph-container methods of Kleitman--Winston and Sapozhenko to reduce the possible obstructions to a small enough family to permit a union bound.

</details>


### [7] [Double-Partition Coloring problem: a unified approach for conflict-free coloring of hypergraphs and other coloring problems in graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09154)
*Mauro Lucci,Graciela Nasini,Paola Tolomei,Luis Miguel Torres*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究双分区着色问题（DPCP）的结构与计算性质，提出精确与启发式求解方法，包括结构性质、启发式策略及增强的分支定价算法，实验表明分支定价与紧凑整数规划公式互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPCP是图与超图上多种着色问题（如分区着色、列表着色、无冲突着色CFCP）的推广，具有重要理论意义；但其结构与计算性质尚待深入研究，且缺乏有效的精确求解方法，尤其CFCP实例上尚无精确算法的计算评估。

Method: 1) 推导结构性质，用于检测不可行实例并缩减问题规模；2) 提出一步与两步启发式以获得高质量解；3) 基于集合覆盖公式构建增强的分支定价算法，并引入求解NP难定价问题的新策略；4) 采用紧凑整数规划公式进行对比。

Result: 计算实验显示，分支定价算法与紧凑整数规划公式互补：紧凑公式在稀疏实例上更有效，分支定价在更稠密、更大规模实例上表现更优；且这些是首批在各种CFCP实例上计算评估的精确算法。

Conclusion: 所提结构性质、启发式与增强分支定价算法有效求解DPCP，并与紧凑公式形成互补；首次实现CFCP实例上的精确算法计算评估，为后续研究提供方法基础。

Abstract: The Double-Partition Coloring Problem (DPCP) is a recently introduced generalization of several coloring problems on graphs and hypergraphs, including the Partition Coloring Problem, the List Coloring Problem, and the Conflict-Free Coloring Problem (CFCP). In this work, we further investigate the structural and computational properties of the DPCP and develop exact and heuristic solution approaches. We derive structural results that allow the detection of infeasible instances and the reduction of their size. We propose one-step and two-step heuristics for obtaining high-quality solutions. These procedures are incorporated into an enhanced branch-and-price algorithm based on a set covering formulation of the DPCP. The algorithm further includes new strategies for solving the NP-hard pricing problem. Computational experiments show that the proposed branch-and-price algorithm and a compact integer programming formulation complement each other: while the compact formulation is generally more effective on sparse instances, branch-and-price performs particularly well on denser and larger instances. In particular, these are the first exact algorithms to be computationally evaluated on various CFCP instances.

</details>


### [8] [On graphs whose powers are well-covered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09300)
*My Hanh Pham,Thanh Vu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究所有幂均为良覆盖图的图类，并对弦图、余弦图及强良覆盖图给出完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的幂的良覆盖性质在组合图论中具有重要性，但此前缺乏对哪些图的所有幂都良覆盖的系统刻画，尤其缺少对弦图、余弦图、强良覆盖图等基本图类的完整分类。

Method: 针对"所有幂都良覆盖"这一性质，逐一分析弦图、余弦图以及强良覆盖图这三类图，通过结构分析完成完整分类。

Result: 获得了弦图、余弦图和强良覆盖图中所有幂均为良覆盖图的图的完整分类结果。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了在若干经典图类中"所有幂都良覆盖"的图的精确结构，为良覆盖图与图幂理论的结合提供了完整分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate graphs with the property that all of their powers are well-covered. We obtain complete classifications for several graph classes, including chordal graphs, cochordal graphs, and very well-covered graphs.

</details>


### [9] [A near-quadratic lower bound for sets with no unique sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09349)
*Jianfeng Hou,Kai Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 中"每个和都有两种不同无序对表示（允许重复）"的最小子集大小 $m(p)$，证明了对于所有素数 $p\ge64$，$m(p)\ge 2^{-80}(\log p/\log\log p)^2$，并构造上界 $m(p)\le (\log p)^2/(2(\log 3)^2)+(1/(4\log 3)+o(1))(\log p)^2/\log\log p$，从而得到 $m(p)=(\log p)^{2+o(1)}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 上满足"每个和至少有两种不同无序对表示（允许重复）"的最小子集大小的渐近阶。该量刻画了所谓弱三元平衡（weakly ternary-balanced）集合的极端尺度，与加法组合、碰撞格（collision lattice）及有限域上的表示问题相关；此前 $m(p)$ 的阶未被确定。

Method: 下界：对最小集合大小 $n$ 的整数碰撞格（integer collision lattice）做单位主元消元（unit-pivot elimination）压缩；利用共享随机样本与有界直径森林，将存活坐标缩减到 $O(\sqrt n+n/\log p)$ 个且高度为多项式；再通过一个被 $p$ 整除的非零子式导出下界。上界：构造弱三元平衡种子（weakly ternary-balanced seeds）给出显式构造。

Result: 对每个素数 $p\ge64$，$m(p)\ge 2^{-80}(\log p/\log\log p)^2$；并证明 $m(p)\le (\log p)^2/(2(\log 3)^2)+(1/(4\log 3)+o(1))(\log p)^2/\log\log p$。因此 $m(p)=(\log p)^{2+o(1)}$（$p$ 沿素数趋于无穷）。

Conclusion: 确定了 $m(p)$ 的渐近阶为 $(\log p)^{2+o(1)}$，但常数因子阶仍未确定。

Abstract: Let $m(p)$ be the least size of a subset of $\F_p$ with at least two elements for which every sum has two distinct representations as unordered pairs, allowing repetition. We prove that, for every prime $p\ge64$, \[
  m(p)\ge2^{-80}\left(\frac{\log p}{\log\log p}\right)^2. \] The argument compresses the full integer collision lattice by unit-pivot elimination. A shared random sample and forests of bounded diameter give $O(\sqrt n+n/\log p)$ surviving coordinates of polynomial height for a minimal set of size $n$. A nonzero minor divisible by $p$ then gives the lower bound. We also construct weakly ternary-balanced seeds yielding \[
  m(p)\le\frac{(\log p)^2}{2(\log3)^2}
  +\left(\frac1{4\log3}+o(1)\right)
  \frac{(\log p)^2}{\log\log p}. \] Consequently $m(p)=(\log p)^{2+o(1)}$ as $p$ tends to infinity through the primes. The constant-factor order of $m(p)$ remains undetermined.

</details>

Abstract: The $r$th-order Stirling cycle and subset triangles and their associated quasi-Eulerian triangles were introduced by Deb and Sokal in their study of total positivity of combinatorial triangles. For $r\ge 2$, we resolve this problem by introducing the notion of Stirling subset permutations along with a consecutive-descent statistic. Our log-concavity proofs rely on Sagan's criterion, Dey's extension, and strengthened log-concavity inequalities discovered with the assistance of ChatGPT 5.6.

Abstract: Kühn and Osthus proved that, for every positive integer $\ell$, every $2^{16}\ell^2$-connected graph $G$ has a partition $V(G)=S\cup T$ such that $G[S]$ and $G[T]$ are $\ell$-connected and $d_T(v)\geq \ell$ for every $v\in S$. In this paper, we answer this question in the affirmative by proving that every $641\ell$-connected graph $G$ has a partition $V(G)=S\cup T$ such that $G[S]$ and $G[T]$ are $\ell$-connected and $d_T(v)\ge \ell$ for every $v\in S$. The proof integrates the Moser-Tardos resampling algorithm.

Abstract: We study the uniqueness of optimal $1$-embeddings on closed surfaces up to weak equivalence. Answering a question of Suzuki in the negative, we show that for every closed surface other than the sphere, the projective plane and the Klein bottle, there exists an optimal $1$-embedded graph admitting two weakly inequivalent optimal $1$-embeddings. Our construction is given explicitly on the torus and is then extended to other surfaces through a suitable connected-sum construction that preserves weak inequivalence. TL;DR: 本文解决了Cameron、Chaplick和Hoàng提出的公开问题，给出了连通弦$\llcorner$-EPG图的两个等价刻画。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画弦$\llcorner$-EPG图是Cameron等人明确提出的公开问题，此前缺乏对该图类的结构性理解。

Method: 第一个刻画为每个连通弦$\llcorner$-EPG图赋予其顶点集的有序团划分树，其底层树为水平与垂直边相交分量的二部关联图；第二个刻画仅用极大团表述，即连通弦图是$\llcorner$-EPG当且仅当某个团树容许一个可容许部分定向。通过归约引理将局部团标签替换为极大团，连接两个刻画，并将可容许部分定向的存在性归约为2-SAT，在$O(nM^2)$时间内判定。

Result: 连通弦$\llcorner$-EPG图可由有序团划分树刻画，也可由团树上的可容许部分定向刻画；对给定团树，判定问题归约为2-SAT并在$O(nM^2)$时间内求解；强弦性不充分，给出一个强弦图作为$\llcorner$-EPG的极小禁导出子图，并对具有唯一团树的裂图族给出隶属判定归约为邻域条件的结论。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了连通弦$\llcorner$-EPG图，提供了高效判定算法，并界定了弦性与强弦性在该图类中的充分性边界。

Abstract: A $k$-bend path is a non-self-intersecting polyline that lies on a grid and consists of at most $k+1$ axis-parallel line segments. An $\llcorner$-EPG graph is a graph whose vertices can be represented by 1-bend paths on a grid, where each path is either $\llcorner$, $\shortmid$, or $\text{-}$, such that two vertices are adjacent if and only if the corresponding paths share at least one grid edge. Characterizing chordal $\llcorner$-EPG graphs was explicitly posed as an open problem by Cameron, Chaplick, and Hoàng. We resolve this by giving two equivalent characterizations of connected chordal $\llcorner$-EPG graphs. The first assigns to every connected chordal $\llcorner$-EPG graph an ordered clique partition tree of its vertex set, where the underlying tree is the bipartite incidence graph of the horizontal and vertical edge-intersection components. The second is stated purely in terms of maximal cliques: a connected chordal graph is $\llcorner$-EPG if and only if some clique tree admits an admissible partial orientation. A reduction lemma replaces local clique labels by maximal cliques, linking the two characterizations. For a prescribed clique tree, the existence of an admissible partial orientation reduces to 2-SAT and is decided in $O(nM^2)$ time, where $n$ and $M$ are the numbers of vertices and maximal cliques. Finally, we show that strong chordality is not sufficient: we exhibit a strongly chordal graph that is a minimal forbidden induced subgraph for $\llcorner$-EPG, and a family of split graphs with a unique clique tree for which membership in $\llcorner$-EPG reduces to a neighborhood condition.

</details>

Method: 引入单项式Cayley图（monomial Cayley graphs），允许的单位步长取单个复数参数的带符号幂；对超越参数与单位根分别计算染色数和圆染色数；对代数非整数参数，利用其本原整数最小多项式首项系数进行界定。

Conclusion: 提出猜想：对任意代数非整数方向参数三色足够，对任意方向参数四色足够。

Abstract: How do algebraic relations among unit directions influence the chromatic number of the plane? We introduce monomial Cayley graphs as a framework for investigating this question, beginning with graphs whose allowed unit steps are signed powers of a single complex parameter. Transcendental parameters give bipartite graphs, while for roots of unity we determine both the chromatic and circular chromatic numbers. As a geometric consequence, every graph admitting a unit-distance embedding in the plane in which the angle between any two edge directions is a rational multiple of $π$ is three-colorable. For algebraic noninteger parameters, four colors suffice, and three suffice when the primitive integer minimal polynomial has leading coefficient at least three. We conjecture that three colors suffice for every algebraic noninteger direction parameter and that four suffice for every direction parameter.

Abstract: Let $f(d)$ be the smallest integer such that every finite connected bridgeless simple graph of diameter $d$ admits a strong orientation of diameter at most $f(d)$. ### [18] [Melnikov's Valency Variety Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09649)

Conclusion: Melnikov提出的严格不等式不成立；本文给出的两个下界均为最优，并完整解决了Melnikov的度多样性问题。

Abstract: Let $w(G)$ denote the number of distinct vertex degrees of a finite simple graph $G$. Melnikov asked for a lower bound on the chromatic number $χ(G)$ in terms of $|V(G)|$ and $w(G)$, and conjectured a strict bound of this type. We resolve Melnikov's valency-variety problem by proving that every graph $G$ with at least two vertices satisfies
  \begin{equation*} χ(G)\ge\left\lceil1+\frac{2w(G)(w(G)-1)}{4w(G)(|V(G)|-w(G))+(|V(G)|-w(G)-1)^2}\right\rceil,
  \end{equation*}
  and consequently
  \begin{equation*}
  χ(G)\ge\left\lceil\frac{\lfloor w(G)/2\rfloor}{|V(G)|-w(G)}\right\rceil.
  \end{equation*} Finally, we also construct an explicit infinite family of graphs attaining equality in both bounds. In particular, these examples show that Melnikov's proposed strict inequality is false and that the bounds above are best possible.

</details>

Abstract: We study two graph pebbling parameters, the stacking number and the clearing number, through the Almost Stacked Hypothesis (ASH). This hypothesis asserts that these thresholds can be determined by testing only configurations in which at most one vertex carries more than one pebble. We prove that every almost stacked configuration of size $2^{n+1}-1$ on $C_{2n}$ is stackable and that every almost stacked configuration of size $3\cdot 2^n-2$ on $C_{2n+1}$ is clearable. Together with the known lower bounds, these results show that ASH implies $\operatorname{stack}(C_{2n})=2^{n+1}-1$ and $\operatorname{clear}(C_{2n+1})=3\cdot 2^n-2$. For a finite tree $T$, we introduce an explicit invariant $\operatorname{estim}(T)$. We prove unconditionally that $\operatorname{stack}(T)\geq\operatorname{estim}(T)$ and prove the reverse inequality under ASH. Consequently, ASH yields $\operatorname{stack}(T)=\operatorname{estim}(T)$, and we conjecture that this equality holds unconditionally. Finally, we study perfectly pebblable graphs: finite connected non-bipartite graphs whose clearing number has the minimum possible value $\operatorname{clear}(G)=|V(G)|+1$. Every complete graph with at least three vertices is perfectly pebblable, which might suggest that perfect pebblability requires high edge density. Assuming ASH, however, we show that this is not the case. We give a sufficient criterion involving strong edge-triangulation and Hamiltonian-path and path-cover conditions in vertex-deleted subgraphs and use it to construct two explicit infinite families of perfectly pebblable graphs with edge density tending to zero, one of which has only a linear number of edges.

</details>

Abstract: Let $k\ge2$ be a fixed integer. Abstract: We develop Delsarte theory for quantum association schemes, allowing both composition and the quantum Schur product to be noncommutative. Abstract: Start with the number $2$. At each move, combine two numbers already made, but the operations must be used in the fixed repeating order $+,\times,-,÷$. Conventional induction cannot work, because a division that comes right after a completed interval $\{1,\dots,P\}$ produces numbers already made. Instead we extend a completed interval $\{1,\dots,P\}$ to $\{1,\dots,3P\}$ all at once, which counting shows is the smallest multiplicative extension $P\to kP$ that can work, and then adjust the last few moves to reach every other $N$. For $9\le N\le 33$ we give explicit sequences, found by computer search.

</details>

When \(f^2(1,k)=f^2(k,k)\), this sequence admits a Chebyshev reduction to \( U_{j+1}(x)+δU_j(x)=0, \) covering \(L/L^+\) and \(A_α\); explicit cosine spectra occur only in special cases, such as the adjacency matrix and the \(L/L^+\) end factor of \(B_{n,k}\). We derive positive-semidefinite energy formulas, Gershgorin criteria, spectral-gap estimates, interlacing and non-interlacing results, partial eigenvalue-coincidence information, and McClelland- and Koolen--Moulton-type bounds. Several known results are recovered as special cases of this unified framework.

Abstract: We introduce the Domination Reciprocal Mean Square index \(DRMS\), a new domination-degree-based topological index obtained by replacing the ordinary degree in the reciprocal mean square index with the domination degree. Abstract: We introduce general methods to derive lower tail large deviation principles, for a variety of nonlinear problems of combinatorial interest. These methods are especially effective in "critical" regimes, where the nonlinearities are not sparse enough for Poissonian lower tails (e.g., Janson's inequality does not provide a sharp tail bound), but the situation is not so dense that combinatorial effects dominate (e.g., we cannot directly apply the theory of hypergraph containers or the relative entropy framework of Kozma-Samotij). In particular, these are regimes where one expects interesting phase transitions to occur.
  Our results have a number of consequences related to subgraphs in random graphs, answering various questions of Warnke and Jenssen-Perkins-Potukuchi-Simkin. For example, consider a random graph $G\sim \mathbb G(n,p)$, and let $L_\triangle(n,p)=\log\Pr[G\text{ is triangle-free}]$. The first-order asymptotics of $L_\triangle(n,p)$ have long been known in all parameter ranges except the critical regime where $p$ has order of magnitude $1/\sqrt n$. We are able to fill this gap: for any fixed $c>0$, we show that $n^{-3/2}L_\triangle(n,c/\sqrt n)$ converges to a limit expressed in terms of a two-parameter variational problem, which has a single phase transition at $c\approx 4.341$. In contrast, we show that there is no such phase transition for the probability that $G$ is $C_{2\ell}$-free, for any fixed even cycle $C_{2\ell}$.
  We also obtain some applications in combinatorial design theory. Addressing conjectures of Glock-Kühn-Lo-Osthus, Kelly, and Kwan-Sah-Sawhney-Simkin, we obtain new estimates on the number of order-$n$ Steiner triple systems with no Pasch configuration and the number of order-$n$ Latin squares with no $2\times 2$ Latin subsquare (both tight up to a factor of $\exp(o(n^2))$).

Abstract: For a digraph $D$, let $α_2(D)$ be the largest size of a vertex set containing no directed $2$-cycle. Let $f_2(a)$ be the least positive integer $k$ such that every $k$-strongly connected digraph $D$ with $α_2(D)\le a$ has a Hamilton cycle. Jackson and Ordaz conjectured that $f_2(a)\le a+1$. We also disprove the pancyclicity conjecture of Jackson and Ordaz that every digraph $D$ with $κ(D)\geα_2(D)+1$ contains a directed cycle of every length from $2$ to $|V(D)|$.

Abstract: Over the past few decades, several notions of graph curvature have been introduced. Among them, Ollivier curvature, defined through optimal transport, and its Lin--Lu--Yau variant provide notions of curvature for graphs which retain several features of their counterpart in Riemannian geometry. A classical problem in this context is to understand the global structure of positively curved spaces.

Abstract: How large of a minimum degree does an $n$-vertex graph need before we are sure that it contains a perfect matching? Dirac's theorem states that a graph on an even number of vertices in which each vertex has degree at least $n/2$ has this property. In this short expository note, intended to be used in the classroom, we discuss how this statement generalizes to hypergraphs. In particular, we highlight an elegant connection between fractional perfect matchings in hypergraphs and a probabilistic inequality about nonnegative random variables, which was conjectured by Feige. We also present a very short self-contained proof of Feige's conjecture.

We accomplish this by A) utilizing a recent result showing that the regionally proximal relation is an equivalence relation for minimal actions of commutative semigroups and by B) describing a new, DeMorgan-type algebra on Furstenberg families that allows for efficient manipulation and computation. Abstract: We study the domains of absolute convergence of monomial expansions of holomorphic functions on Banach sequence spaces. The proof is based on an idea of Gleit and Lazar, who established an analogous result for the space of real $n \times n$ matrices equipped with either the trace-class norm or the operator norm

Abstract: A conference signal is a complex-valued function on a finite abelian group that vanishes at 0 in both the time and frequency domains, and is otherwise flat in both domains. For example, the Legendre symbol is a conference signal on $\mathbb{Z}_p$ since it vanishes at $0$, takes values $\pm 1$ away from zero, and is a scalar multiple of its Fourier transform. More generally, any nontrivial multiplicative character on a finite field is a conference signal on its additive group.
  Until now, conference signals have mainly been studied by number theorists, who wanted to know if multiplicative characters were the only conference signals on finite fields. The state of the art uses fancy tools from symplectic geometry to prove that non-character conference signals exist on non-small fields. The same tools show conference signals exist on every abelian group of odd order, without saying what they are.
  In this (human-friendly!) paper, we explain how conference signals produce other objects of interest in algebraic combinatorics and applied harmonic analysis, and then we present some new, explicit constructions of conference signals on $\mathbb{Z}_n$. In particular, we give the first explicit conference signals for composite $n$ and the first explicit non-character examples for prime $n$.

Abstract: We study continuous embeddings from two classes of generalized weighted Morrey spaces into generalized Morrey spaces. As a consequence, we characterize the corresponding embeddings in the radial setting for the full range $0<p_1,p_2<\infty$. We then study embeddings from generalized Komori--Shirai-type weighted Morrey spaces associated with piecewise power weights and general Muckenhoupt weights. For the Samko-type scale, we treat both generalized and classical weighted Morrey spaces, again considering piecewise power weights and general Muckenhoupt weights. Finally, assuming that the weight is comparable to a positive constant on some cube or ball, we show that the continuous embeddings under consideration are not compact.

Abstract: Let $E$ and $F$ be separable symmetric Banach sequence spaces, and let $C_E$ and $C_F$ be the corresponding ideals of compact operators on a separable Hilbert space. We prove that if $C_F$ is isomorphic to a complemented subspace of $S_E$, then $F=\ell_2$. As a consequence, if $C_E$ is isomorphic to $C_F$, then $E=F$ with equivalent norms. This answers Pełczyński's 1979 question concerning the uniqueness of symmetric structure. We also strengthen Arazy's result by showing that if $E$ has finite cotype and $C_F$ embeds isomorphically into $S_E$, then $F=\ell_2$. For reflexive $F\ne\ell_2$ with non-trivial Boyd indices, the embedding theorems of Lindenstrauss and Szankowski yield a separable reflexive symmetric sequence space $E$ with trivial cotype such that $C_F$ embeds isomorphically into $S_E$. This gives negative answers to several questions raised by Arazy in 1981.

</details>

*Alexander Dobrick*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了复Banach格上任意正算子的边缘谱是循环的，无条件解决了循环性猜想，并给出Lotz定理的新证明。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于Vesentini谱半径次调和性导出谱支配定理：同谱半径且一算子支配另一算子时，小算子边缘谱含于大算子边缘谱中。通过格扩张与基于挠算子的平均程序构造保持谱半径、渐近旋转自相似的递减正minorant序列，再用超幂论证将渐近关系精确化为旋转自相似，从而由谱支配推出循环性。

Conclusion: 该工作以谱支配为核心统一处理边缘谱循环性问题，无需增长假设，为后续研究提供了可推广的方法框架。

Abstract: We prove that the peripheral spectrum of every positive operator on a complex Banach lattice is cyclic, thereby resolving the cyclicity conjecture without additional growth assumptions. Our proof is based on a spectral domination theorem derived from Vesentini's theorem on the subharmonicity of the spectral radius: when two positive operators have the same spectral radius and one dominates the other, the peripheral spectrum of the smaller operator is contained in that of the larger one. This extends an earlier result of Räbiger and Wolff. After passing to a suitable lattice extension, an averaging procedure based on torsion operators produces a decreasing sequence of positive minorants which retain the spectral radius and become asymptotically rotationally self-similar. An ultrapower argument turns this asymptotic relation into exact rotational self-similarity, allowing us to deduce cyclicity from spectral domination. The same approach also yields an alternative proof of Lotz's cyclicity theorem for Abel solvable operators.

</details>

Abstract: We study the fundamental groups of a normal projective Calabi-Yau variety $X$ with klt singularities and of its regular locus $X_{reg}$. Abstract: We study global and variational properties of $\mathbb R$-equivariant minimal Lagrangian surfaces in the complex quadric $Q_2$ and the complex hyperbolic quadric $Q_2^*$ arising from loop group potentials. Abstract: A collection of $m \geq 2$ lines through the origin in $\mathbb{R}^2$ is balanced if the sum of the orthogonal projections onto these lines equals $m/2$ times the identity. Abstract: We establish an almost-splitting theorem for spacetimes under timelike sectional curvature bounds in the spirit of the celebrated Riemannian result due to Cheeger and Colding. Abstract: We give an explicit description of the orbits of the split real form $G_{2(2)}$ on the complex flag manifolds of $G_2$ and study their induced invariant CR structures. Abstract: In the upper half-space model of the hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n+1}$, a translation hypersurface of type I is a graph $x_{n+1}=f_1(x_1)+\cdots+f_n(x_n)$ and a translation hypersurface of type II is a graph $x_n=f_1(x_1)+\cdots+f_{n-1}(x_{n-1})+g(x_{n+1})$. We obtain these results as particular cases of a classification of the singular minimal translation hypersurfaces of $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ for an arbitrary exponent $α\neq 0$, with respect to a direction orthogonal (type I) or parallel (type II) to the hyperplane over which the hypersurface is a graph. The minimal translation hypersurfaces of type I are exactly the parabolic cylinders over a one-parameter family of arches, which contradicts a nonexistence theorem in the literature. The minimal translation hypersurfaces of type II are the parabolic cylinders, the vertical totally geodesic hyperplanes and, when $n\geq 3$, the vertical cylinders over the Scherk surface.

Abstract: This is a continuation of our previous paper on toric gravitational instantons. We develop a systematic theory of compact simply-connected Einstein four-manifolds with toric symmetry. Applications include

The key idea involves a particular design of rod structures to enable a gluing construction and restrict possible degenerations.
  (2) A topological characterization of algebraically special toric Einstein metrics. This uses special curvature identities for $W^+$ in the toric setting.
  (3) Uniqueness, up to scaling and isometry, on toric four-manifolds admitting algebraically special Einstein metrics. The proofs uses a variational study of the Einstein--Hilbert functional.
  (4) Diffeomorphism classification of compact toric Einstein four-manifolds in terms of an improved Hitchin-Thorpe inequality $χ\geq3|τ|$.

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a sharp dynamical isoperimetric principle for weak mean curvature flow. More precisely, for the level set flow $K_t$ starting from the boundary of a smooth bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb R^{n+1}$, as well as for the integral Brakke flow $\{μ_t\}$ with initial Radon measure $μ_0=\mathcal H^n\llcorner\partialΩ$, we establish the corresponding optimal extinction time estimates \begin{equation*}

</details>

Abstract: We determine the sharp compactness ranges for the scalar-flat and minimal-boundary Yamabe problems on smooth compact manifolds of positive conformal type, excluding the conformal round hemisphere. For zero scalar curvature and positive constant boundary mean curvature, compactness holds through dimension $14$ for general boundary and dimension $21$ for umbilic boundary. For positive scalar curvature and zero boundary mean curvature, the corresponding upper dimensions are $14$ and $20$. Together with the noncompactness examples in Part I, these results identify the transition dimensions in both boundary classes. Abstract: We study the regularity of eigenfunctions of the Dirichlet Laplacian on domains with corners. The specific question we consider is whether the eigenfunctions can be smooth, e.g. trigonometric, on the closure of their domain. We determine how this regularity depends on the local geometry of the corner. Abstract: We study graph transformations and their effects on the eigenvectors of three fundamental graph matrices: the adjacency matrix $A$, the signless Laplacian $Q$, and the Laplacian $L$. We also extend some of the known $L$ graph transformations to $Q$ and $A$ and prove that graphs admitting a common eigenvector $\mathbf{x}$ for $A, L$ and $Q$ are such that vertices corresponding to nonzero components of $\mathbf{x}$ have same degree. Abstract: We investigate the spectral statistics of random perturbations of semiclassical Schrödinger operators on compact manifolds, near energy levels corresponding to chaotic classical dynamics. The prototypical example is that of an operator $h^2Δ+V(x)$ that is perturbed by a smooth potential $h^αV_ω$ with $α\in (0,1)$, and $V_ω$ is a random potential that decorrelates on distances $h^β$, with $0<β<2α$. Abstract: We prove a sharp Szegö-Weinberger-type inequality for balanced doubly connected domains in $\mathbb S^2$, $\mathbb R^2$ and $\mathbb H^2$. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bedford–McMullen地毯是典型的自仿集，其自交与重叠结构直接关系到自仿测度的维数、绝对连续性及谱性质。Elekes、Keleti和Máthé于2010年提出相关猜想，但以往结果常依赖对定义基底的算术限制。作者希望去除这些算术假设，在更一般的自仿框架下刻画正测度相交的相似变换。

Method: 设K=K(n,m,Γ)为满足n>m≥2、1<#Γ<nm的Bedford–McMullen地毯，μ为其上严格正权重的自仿测度。作者通过分析自仿测度的自交条件μ(K∩f(K))>0，利用地毯的乘积结构与柱集分解，对相似变换f的正交部分进行分类，证明其必为对角或反对角；进而对等距情形借助正交部分受限的事实导出平移结论。

Result: 证明在无任何定义基底算术限制的条件下，若相似变换f满足μ(K∩f(K))>0，则f的正交部分为对角或反对角矩阵。对于等距变换，进一步证明若μ(K∩f(K))>0，则f(K)与K仅相差一个平移。

Conclusion: 该结果在平面自仿情形下给出了Elekes–Keleti–Máthé猜想的强化形式，并表明正测度自交对相似变换的正交部分具有强约束，且不依赖于基底的算术性质。

Abstract: Let $K=K(n,m,Γ)$ be a Bedford-McMullen carpet with $n>m\geq2$ and $1<\#Γ<nm$, and let $μ$ be a self-affine measure on $K$ with strictly positive weights. We prove that if a similitude $f$ satisfies $μ(K\cap f(K))>0$, then its orthogonal part is diagonal or anti-diagonal. This holds without any arithmetic restrictions on the defining bases. For isometries, we further prove that if $μ(K\cap f(K))>0$ then $f(K)$ and $K$ differ only by a translation, thereby establishing a strengthened form of the 2010 conjecture of Elekes, Keleti and Máthé in this planar self-affine context.

</details>


### [84] [Sparse domination modulo polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09771)
*Francesco Di Plinio*

Main category: math.CA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有稀疏控制（如均振荡型与Conde-Alonso-Lorist-Rey的抵消型）无法同时统一捕捉局部多项式逼近和低于每个稀疏立方体尺度的抵消信息，且定性加权估计无法区分BMO与L∞型检验条件的转变，因此需要一种更精细、定量的稀疏控制框架。

Result: 在齐次情形，这些条件刻画了以A∞特征线性依赖的加权Hardy-Sobolev不等式，该定量依赖可检测Lerner关于Riesz位势逐点界中BMO到L∞检验条件的转变（定性加权估计无法做到）。在非齐次情形，证明了相应的加权不等式，并表明从二阶导数起严格弱于逐点稀疏控制。

Conclusion: 该理论统一并推广了现有稀疏控制框架，给出刻画Calderón-Zygmund算子与加权Hardy-Sobolev不等式的定量条件，其推论包括Banach范围以下的Hardy-Sobolev估计、尖锐加权Sobolev不等式以及Riesz位势的逐点界。

Abstract: We develop a sparse domination theory that captures both local polynomial approximation and cancellation of the input below the scale of each sparse cube. Its coefficients are percentiles of localized smooth maximal functions of the input minus its local polynomial approximation, combining features of mean-oscillation sparse bounds and of the cancellative sparse bounds of Conde-Alonso, Lorist and Rey. Using wavelet representations, we characterize the Calderón-Zygmund operators whose derivatives admit these bounds by explicit polynomial testing conditions. In the homogeneous setting, the same conditions characterize weighted Hardy-Sobolev inequalities with linear dependence on the $A_\infty$ characteristic. This quantitative dependence detects the transition from BMO to $L^\infty$ testing conditions that Lerner found for pointwise bounds by Riesz potentials, a distinction that qualitative weighted estimates cannot recover. In the inhomogeneous setting, we prove corresponding weighted inequalities and show that they are strictly weaker than pointwise sparse domination from the second derivative order onward. Consequences include Hardy-Sobolev estimates below the Banach range, sharp weighted Sobolev inequalities, and pointwise bounds by Riesz potentials.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1982年Ismail与Miller猜想：当 $\nu>\mu\geq0$、$b>a>0$ 时，函数 $x\mapsto G(x;\mu,\nu)$ 是某无穷可分概率分布的Laplace变换，该猜想等价于涉及Bessel函数零点 $j_{\nu,n}$ 的指数和关于阶的单调性。本文旨在进一步将问题归约并加以证明。

Method: 将原问题归约为 $Q_\nu(t)=\sum_{n\geq1}j_{\nu,n}^2e^{-t j_{\nu,n}^2}$ 关于 $\nu$ 的单调性；证明的关键工具是关于阶的修正Bessel函数之商导数的积分表示，并结合Bernstein定理与概率尾部论证。

Result: 证明了 $Q_\nu(t)$ 关于 $\nu$ 的单调性，进而确认 $x\mapsto G(x;\mu,\nu)$ 确为无穷可分概率分布的Laplace变换，即Ismail-Miller猜想成立。

Conclusion: Ismail-Miller猜想得到完整证明；所建立的积分表示与Bernstein定理、尾部概率方法为相关特殊函数单调性问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Mourad E.H. Ismail and Kenneth S. Miller in 1982 conjectured that for $ν>μ\geq0$ and $b>a>0$ the function $$x\mapsto G(x;μ,ν)=\left(\frac ba\right)^{μ-ν}\frac{I_μ(a\sqrt{x})I_ν(b\sqrt{x})}{I_μ(b\sqrt{x})I_ν(a\sqrt{x})},$$ where $I_ν$ stands for the modified Bessel function of the first kind, is the Laplace transform of an infinitely divisible probability distribution. Their conjecture is equivalent to the monotonicity property of some exponential sums over the zeros $j_{ν,n}$ with respect to the order, where $j_{ν,n}$ denotes the $n$th positive zero of the Bessel function of the first kind of order $ν$. In this paper our aim is to reduce the problem further to the monotonicity, with respect to $ν$, of \[Q_ν(t)=\sum_{n\geq1}j_{ν,n}^2e^{-t j_{ν,n}^2},\qquad t>0,\] and to prove this monotonicity. In this way we show that indeed the above function $x\mapsto G(x;μ,ν)$ is the Laplace transform of an infinitely divisible probability distribution, and the above conjecture is true. The key ingredient in our proof is an integral representation for the derivative of a quotient of modified Bessel functions of the first kind with respect to the order, combined with Bernstein's theorem and a probability tail argument.

</details>

*Lidia Fernández,Juan Antonio Villegas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展的Koornwinder型方法构造多元多重正交多项式，通过两种耦合方案生成Type I/II双变量及Type II多变量系统，并在多种二维/高维区域给出显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元多重正交多项式（MOPs）框架中，缺乏在多种多维区域上生成结构化MOPs族的统一机制。

Method: 发展扩展的Koornwinder型方法；引入两种耦合方案：(1) 单变量MOPs族与标准正交族耦合，定义Type I与Type II双变量系统及其对偶双正交关系；(2) 两个单变量MOPs族耦合形成Type II多变量系统。

Result: 首次给出多类二维区域的MOPs显式表达式：抛物域、正方形[0,1]^2、正象限(R_0^+)^2、楔形域V^2、三角形T；并给出d维单纯形T^d与d维锥V^d上的显式多重正交系统。

Conclusion: 所提Koornwinder型统一框架可系统构造多元MOPs，扩展了MOPs在多种多维区域上的显式理论。

Abstract: In the framework of multiple orthogonal polynomials (MOPs) and their extension to the multivariate setting, a unified mechanism for generating structured families of multiple orthogonal polynomials across diverse multidimensional domains is still lacking. In this work, we bridge this gap by developing an extended Koornwinder-type methodology for constructing multivariate multiple orthogonal polynomials. We introduce two distinct coupling schemes: combining a univariate MOPs family with a standard orthogonal family, which enables the definition of both Type I and Type II bivariate systems along with their dual biorthogonality relations, and coupling two univariate MOPs families to form Type II multivariate systems.
  Using this general framework, we provide the first explicit formulations of multiple orthogonal polynomials on a variety of bivariate regions, including bounded domains such as a parabolic domain and the square $[0,1]^2$, non-standard unbounded geometries like the positive quadrant $(\mathbb{R}_0^+)^2$ and the wedge $\mathbb{V}^2$, as well as new constructions on the triangle $T$. Furthermore, explicit multiple orthogonal systems on the $d$-dimensional simplex $T^d$ and on the $d$-dimensional cone $\mathbb{V}^d$ are given.

</details>


### [87] [Bounds on the maximum number of limit cycles of piecewise linear Lienard systems I. The continuous case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10050)
*Shiyu Cai,Hebai Chen,Jie Jin,Yuhuan Lu*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究折点数恰为 n 的连续分段线性 F(x) 的平面 Liénard 系统 \dot x=y-F(x), \dot y=-x，证明极限环最大数目至少为 |3n-4|，从而对 n≥3 否证了 Tonnelier 猜想，并给出有限上界 2^{28(n+1)^2}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tonnelier 猜想该系统的极限环最大数目恰为折点数 n，此前仅对 n=1、n=2 得到证实，n≥3 仍为公开问题。本文旨在处理这一悬而未决的情形，判定猜想对更大 n 是否成立，并刻画多极限环的生成与共存机制。

Method: 针对 F 为恰含 n 个折点的连续分段线性函数的平面 Liénard 系统，构造并分析多尺度扰动，揭示多个极限环生成与共存的内在机制；同时建立极限环数目的有限上界估计。

Result: 证明极限环最大数目至少为 |3n-4|（n∈N^+），由此对 n≥3 否证 Tonnelier 猜想；并证明极限环数目存在有限上界 2^{28(n+1)^2}。

Conclusion: Tonnelier 猜想在 n≥3 时不成立，最大极限环数可随折点数近似线性增长（至少 |3n-4|），上限为 2^{28(n+1)^2}；其证明揭示了统一的多尺度扰动机制。

Abstract: This paper concerns the planar Liénard system \(\dot x=y-F(x),\ \dot y=-x\), where \(F(x)\) is a continuous piecewise linear function with exactly \(n\) fold points. Tonnelier [SIAM J. Appl. Math. 63 (2002)] conjectured that the maximum number of limit cycles of the system is \(n\) when \(F\) has exactly \(n\) fold points. The conjecture was confirmed for \(n=1\) and \(n=2\) in [J. Nonlinear Sci. 25 (2015)] and [J. Lond. Math. Soc. 113 (2026)], respectively, whereas the case \(n\ge3\) remained open. In this paper, we show that the maximal number of limit cycles is at least \(|3n-4|\) for \(n\in\mathbb N^+\), thereby disproving Tonnelier's conjecture for \(n\ge3\). The proof reveals a unified multiscale perturbation mechanism underlying the creation and coexistence of multiple limit cycles. Moreover, we prove that the number of limit cycles admits the finite upper bound \(2^{28(n+1)^2}\).

</details>


### [88] [On $L^p$ norms of maximal operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10247)
*Valentina Ciccone,Mahdi Hormozi,José Madrid,Jakub Niksiński,Błażej Wróbel*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究若干极大算子在 \(L^p\) 上的精确范数，证明在紧性条件下正对称次马尔可夫半群极大算子范数为 \(p'=p/(p-1)\)，并将其应用于热、Poisson、Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 等半群及中心 Hardy–Littlewood 极大算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和分析、半群理论与概率论中许多极大算子的 \(L^p\) 精确范数（尤其是是否等于 \(p'\)）长期受到关注；作者希望在统一框架下刻画使极大 \(L^p\) 范数等于 \(p'\) 的条件，并据此处理经典与半群型极大算子。

Method: 对非原子 \(\sigma\)-有限测度空间上的强连续正对称次马尔可夫半群 \((T_t)\)，在 \(f\mapsto T_{t_0}(\mathbf 1_E f)\) 于 \(L^2\) 紧的假设下，下界通过用有限二进条件期望在合适半群时刻逼近得到，上界使用次马尔可夫形式的 Stein 极大不等式；再结合既有工作研究 \(d\to\infty\) 的极限。

Result: 证明在给定紧性条件下，半群极大算子在每个 \(1<p<\infty\) 上的 \(L^p\) 范数为 \(p'\)；由此给出热、Poisson、Ornstein–Uhlenbeck、Schrödinger、Dunkl、Laguerre、Jacobi 等半群以及 \(\mathbb R\) 上中心 Hardy–Littlewood 极大算子的精确范数；特别地，任意正维数无边完备连通 Riemann 流形上的热与 Poisson 极大算子 \(L^p\) 范数为 \(p'\)，无需曲率或随机完备性假设；并证明 \(p'\) 是 \(B(p,d)\) 当 \(d\to\infty\) 的极限，而 \(d\ge2\) 时该极大算子范数严格大于 \(p'\)。

Conclusion: 在适当紧性条件下，正对称次马尔可夫半群极大算子的 \(L^p\) 范数精确等于 \(p'\)，该结果统一覆盖多种经典半群和 \(\mathbb R\) 上的中心 Hardy–Littlewood 极大算子；同时高维中心 Hardy–Littlewood 极大算子范数严格大于 \(p'\)，但其随维数增大趋于 \(p'\)。

Abstract: We study the exact $L^p$ norms for a number of maximal operators, which are prominent in harmonic analysis, semigroup theory, and probability. We prove that a strongly continuous positive symmetric sub-Markovian semigroup $(T_t)_{t\ge0}$ on a nonatomic $σ$-finite measure space $(X,\mathcal F,μ)$ has maximal $L^p$ norm $p'=p/(p-1)$ for every $1<p<\infty$ whenever $f\mapsto T_{t_0}(\mathbf 1_Ef)$ is compact on $L^2(μ)$ for some $t_0>0$ and some measurable set $E$ of positive measure. The lower bound follows by approximating finite dyadic conditional expectations at suitable semigroup times; the upper bound is Stein's maximal inequality in its sub-Markovian form.
  Important examples of such maximal operators we discuss include maximal functions of various semigroups (heat, Poisson, Ornstein--Uhlenbeck, Schrödinger, Dunkl, Laguerre, Jacobi) as well as the centered Hardy-Littlewood maximal operator on $\mathbb{R}.$ In particular, the heat and Poisson maximal operators on every complete connected Riemannian manifold of positive dimension without boundary have exact $L^p$ norm $p'$, with no curvature or stochastic completeness assumptions.
  Combining our approach with previous work of the first and last authors we also prove that $p'$ is the limit, as $d\to \infty,$ of the $L^p$ norms $B(p,d)$ of the centered Hardy-Littlewood maximal operators on $\mathbb{R}^d.$ On the other hand, we show that the norm of this maximal operator is strictly larger than $p',$ in dimensions $d\ge2.$

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toms-Winter猜想是C*-代数分类理论的核心问题之一，其核心难点在于证明单、可分、纯、核C*-代数的Z-稳定性。作者希望借助II_1因子迹完备C*-代数这一新框架统一处理迹空间上的正则性、K-稳定性与超有限性，从而完成该猜想的最终证明。

Result: 证明了所有单、可分、纯、核C*-代数的Z-稳定性；结合Thiel的工作完成了Toms-Winter猜想；给出迹完备C*-代数正则性的多种等价刻画；证明拟迹为迹的单纯C*-代数是K-稳定的；在可驯情形从正则性条件得到超有限性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过II_1因子迹完备C*-代数的框架，将正则性、K-稳定性与超有限性统一起来，最终证明了单、可分、纯、核C*-代数的Z-稳定性，从而完成了Toms-Winter猜想的证明；同时其局部量子化方法为可驯情形的分类提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove Z-stability for all simple, separable, pure, nuclear C*-algebras. Combining this with very recent work of Thiel completes the proof of the Toms-Winter conjecture.
  We do this through the framework of II_1 factorial tracially complete C*-algebras. We characterise regularity for these algebras in various terms. In particular they are regular if and only if they have real rank zero and the Murray-von Neumann equivalence classes of projections correspond to continuous affine functions from the designated traces to [0,1]. As an application of our analysis of ultrapowers of tracial completions, we show simple pure C*-algebras whose quasitraces are traces are K-stable.
  In the amenable setting we give a local quantisation result, a la Popa, for II_1 factorial amenable tracially complete C*-algebras, which obtains hyperfiniteness from regularity conditions. Our Z-stability results follow from this together with a theorem of Matui and Sato.
  An initial version of an essential lemma in the Bauer trace simplex case was obtained from ChatGPT Astra.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KMS对称量子Markov半群由其诱导的二次形式唯一决定，这类二次形式即量子Dirichlet形式。为理解其结构，需要用自然数学对象（导子）对其作完整刻画。

Method: 将量子Dirichlet形式与导子联系起来，刻画其有界情形，并显式刻画I型因子上有界量子Dirichlet形式；对无界情形给出导子表示。核心技术是分析模群在量子Dirichlet形式定义域上的限制，及其解析生成元平方根的增殖性。

Conclusion: 导子为量子Dirichlet形式提供了统一表示框架，模群在定义域上的性质是连接形式与导子的关键技术工具。

Abstract: A KMS-symmetric quantum Markov semigroup is completely determined by the quadratic form in induces on the GNS Hilbert space. The quadratic forms that arise in this way are called quantum Dirichlet forms. In this article we give a complete description of bounded quantum Dirichlet forms in terms of derivations. In particular, we exhibit an explicit characterization of the bounded quantum Dirichlet forms on type I factors. We also show that any unbounded quantum Dirichlet form can be represented in terms of derivations. The key technical insight is that the modular group always restricts to a strongly continuous group on the domain of a quantum Dirichlet form such that the square root of the analytic generator is accretive with respect to the form norm.

</details>

*Michael Mampusti,Alexander Mundey,Nicholas Seaton*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用Kajiwara-Watatani C*-代数与KMS态理论；引入迭代函数系统的Markov分划并设计构造有限Markov分划的算法；对允许有限Markov分划的压缩系统构造内在Hutchinson测度，该测度满足由集合值动力学决定的自相似积分方程；随后对规范作用的KMS态进行分类。

Abstract: We study iterated function systems with large overlaps via the Kajiwara-Watatani C*-algebra and its KMS states. Our main contributions are threefold. First, we introduce Markov partitions for iterated function systems and give an algorithm that constructs a finite Markov partition whenever one exists. Second, for contractive systems admitting a finite Markov partition, we construct an intrinsic analogue of the Hutchinson measure that is independent of any labelling or weighting of the maps. It is the unique Borel probability measure satisfying a self-similar integral equation dictated by the set-valued dynamics of the system, and it recovers the Hutchinson measure with uniform weights when the system satisfies the open set condition. Finally, we give a complete classification of KMS states for the gauge action on the Kajiwara-Watatani algebra of every such system. The critical inverse temperature is the exponential growth rate of the intrinsic orbits, which can be strictly smaller than the natural logarithm of the number of maps, and the unique KMS state at this temperature is given by our intrinsic Hutchinson measure. These systems include many with large overlaps not covered by the results of Izumi-Kajiwara-Watatani.

</details>

### [94] [The Geometric Arveson-Douglas Conjecture through Veronese Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09836)
*Francesca Arici,Yufan Ge,Dimitris Michail Gerontogiannis*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了几何Arveson-Douglas猜想在投影簇的Veronese嵌入下具有不变性，将猜想约化到定义理想具有二次Gröbner基的投影簇，并得到Douglas基本类猜想在Veronese嵌入下同样不变，进而给出无穷多反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何Arveson-Douglas猜想刻画了投影簇上Toeplitz代数与Schatten类的关系，但已知满足猜想的例子多限于光滑情形或特殊奇异簇；同时Douglas基本类猜想断言Toeplitz扩张实现KK理论中的T-等变对偶，此前缺乏系统性的构造与检验手段。作者希望借助Veronese嵌入这一视角统一处理奇异性、Gröbner基结构与KK-理论性质，从而既扩展猜想的实例，又揭示基本类猜想的边界。

Method: 以投影簇的Veronese嵌入为工具，研究几何Arveson-Douglas猜想在嵌入下的不变性；通过保持相同的Schatten正则性阈值，将一般投影簇的验证约化到定义理想具有二次Gröbner基的情形；并借助子积系统的Toeplitz代数在KK-理论中的等价性分析，考察Douglas基本类猜想在Veronese嵌入下的不变性。

Result: 证明了几何Arveson-Douglas猜想在Veronese嵌入下不变，由此给出新的满足该猜想的奇异簇实例，并将其约化到具有二次Gröbner基的投影簇；构造出Toeplitz代数不与复数KK-等价的子积系统的典范例子；证明了Douglas基本类猜想在Veronese嵌入下不变。

Conclusion: Veronese嵌入为几何Arveson-Douglas猜想与Douglas基本类猜想提供了统一的不变性框架：一方面通过约化到二次Gröbner基情形扩大猜想的证据并给出新的奇异簇实例，另一方面在推广基本类猜想的同时导出无穷多反例，说明该猜想在一般情形下并不成立。

Abstract: We prove invariance of the geometric Arveson-Douglas conjecture under Veronese embeddings of projective varieties. This provides new examples of singular varieties satisfying the conjecture. Furthermore, it reduces the conjecture to projective varieties whose defining ideals admit quadratic Gr{ö}bner bases, by keeping the same Schatten regularity thresholds. Our results lead to canonical examples of subproduct-systems whose Toeplitz algebras are not $KK$-equivalent to the complex numbers. Finally, we show that Douglas' fundamental class conjecture, asserting that the associated Toeplitz extension implements $\mathbb{T}$-equivariant duality in $KK$-theory, is invariant under Veronese embeddings as well. While the latter result bootstraps the fundamental class conjecture, it also leads to infinitely many counterexamples.

</details>


### [95] [Crystallization of the quantum Stiefel manifolds $SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10377)
*Surajit Biswas,Vinay Deshpande,Bipul Saurabh*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究量子齐性空间 $SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1)$ 的晶体化 $C^*$-代数 $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$，刻画其不可约表示、普适性、$K$-群结构，并证明其与原量子 $C^*$-代数同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子群的晶体化（$q \to 0^+$ 极限）是量子代数研究的重要主题，对 $C^*$-代数层面上的晶体化及其不变量（如 $K$-群）的刻画具有基础性意义，本文旨在系统研究 $SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1)$ 晶体化 $C^*$-代数的结构与同构性质。

Method: 引入晶体化 $C^*$-代数 $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$；证明其每个不可约表示是 $q \to 0^+$ 时 $C(SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1))$ 的不可约表示族的极限；由有限生成元与关系给出普适 $C^*$-代数刻画；构造单位本质短正合列链以计算 $K$-群并确定显式生成元；利用齐性 $C^*$-扩张理论证明同构。

Result: 不可约表示作为 $q \to 0^+$ 极限实现；$C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ 是有限生成元与关系给出的普适 $C^*$-代数；计算出其 $K$-群并给出显式生成元；证明 $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1)) \cong C(SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1))$ 对任意 $q \in (0,1)$ 成立。

Conclusion: 晶体化 $C^*$-代数 $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ 完整编码了量子齐性空间 $SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1)$ 的表示论与拓扑不变量，且在 $C^*$-代数层面与其对所有 $q \in (0,1)$ 同构，表明该晶体化是不依赖于形变参数 $q$ 的刚性对象。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the crystallization $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ of the $C^*$-algebra $C(SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1))$. We show that every irreducible representation of $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ arises as the limit, as $q \to 0^+$, of a family of irreducible representations of $C(SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1))$. As a consequence, we prove that $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ is the universal $C^*$-algebra generated by a finite set of generators and relations. We then construct a chain of unital essential short exact sequences of $C^*$-algebras, enabling us to compute the $K$-groups of $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ and determine explicit generators for them. Using the theory of homogeneous $C^*$-extensions, we prove that $C(SO_0(2n+1)/SO_0(2n-1))$ is isomorphic to $C(SO_q(2n+1)/SO_q(2n-1))$ for every $q \in (0,1)$.

</details>


### [96] [The effect of edge deletion on noncommutative distances on graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07718)
*Eric Swartz*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 删除加权图的边（或顶点）反而可能减小 Dirac 算子诱导的非交换（Connes）距离，比值上界为 $2/\sqrt{3}$，且删除位置可任意远离所考察的顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非交换距离 $d^D$ 在图的局部扰动（删边、删点）下的稳定性，反驳"删除边只会增大距离"的直觉，并刻画这一反直觉现象的边界。

Method: 构造加权 $4$-cycle 的最小反例，分析删边后非交换距离 $d^{D'}$ 与 $d^D$ 的比值上确界，并将上界推广至一般图；对 $n$-cycle 给出使距离减小的充要条件。

Result: 对 $4$-cycle 完全确定 $\sup d^D/d^{D'}=2/\sqrt{3}$，且该上界对所有图成立；$n$-cycle 上删边能使距离减小当且仅当 $4\mid n$；被删边/点无需与 $x,y$ 相邻，可在图论距离与加权测地距离上任意远，甚至使 $d^D(x,y)$ 改变任意大倍数。

Conclusion: 非交换距离对图结构扰动极其敏感且非单调，凸显其与经典图距离的本质差异。

Abstract: For a Dirac operator $D$ on a finite weighted graph, let $d^D$ denote the associated noncommutative (Connes) distance. We show that deleting an edge can actually decrease the noncommutative distance between two vertices in the graph. The smallest example of this phenomenon comes from a weighted 4-cycle, and the case of deleting an edge from a weighted 4-cycle is determined completely: if $d^{D'}$ denotes the noncommutative distance in the graph after edge deletion, we prove that $\sup d^D/d^{D'} = 2/\sqrt{3}$ for the $4$-cycle, and further that this upper bound holds for all graphs. On the other hand, we show that deleting an edge can decrease the noncommutative distance between two vertices of an $n$-cycle precisely when $n$ is divisible by $4$.
  In cases when a deleted edge decreases the noncommutative distance between two vertices $x$ and $y$, we show that the deleted edge need not be incident to either of the vertices $x$ or $y$, and in fact the deleted edge can be arbitrarily far from both in the graph-theoretic (number of edges) distance and in the weighted (geodesic) distance. Moreover, deletion of a single vertex arbitrarily far from both $x$ and $y$ in either the graph-theoretic or weighted distance can change $d^D(x,y)$ by an arbitrarily large factor.

</details>

### [97] [Optimal Linear Inviscid Damping and Vorticity Depletion for Non-monotonic shear flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08912)
*Rajendra Beekie,Shan Chen,Hao Jia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为大类谱稳定剪切流在矩形环面上的最优逐点无粘阻尼与涡量耗散给出了证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广CJWZ25中针对Kolmogorov流（cos y）的线性无粘估计到更一般的谱稳定剪切流，并关键性地去掉涡量耗散和无粘阻尼速率中出现的对数修正。

Method: 结合参数化拟解（parametrix）的逐点估计与一个更简单的内问题的显式解，从而清晰地移除对数修正；所用方法与CJWZ25完全不同。

Result: 对大类谱稳定剪切流 b(y) 证明了最优逐点线性无粘阻尼和涡量耗散。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅扩展了现有结果，且通过内问题显式解使对数修正的移除过程变得清晰明了。

Abstract: We give a proof of optimal pointwise linear inviscid damping and vorticity depletion for a large class of spectrally stable shear flows $b(y)$ on the rectangular torus. This extends the linear inviscid estimates in \cite{CJWZ25} for Kolmogorov flow $b(y) =\cos y$ using completely different methods. Crucially, both methods remove an apparent logarithmic correction to the depletion and inviscid damping rates. Our method combines pointwise bounds for a parametrix with an explicit solution of a simpler inner problem which makes the removal of the logarithm clear.

</details>


### [98] [A sharp spectral multiplier theorem on the Engel group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08976)
*Zhicheng Han,Zhipeng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了Engel群上次拉普拉斯算子的谱乘子定理：在尺度不变局部$H^s$条件（$s>2$）下，乘子算子$F(\mathcal L)$是弱$(1,1)$型且在$L^p\ (1<p<\infty)$上有界，从而将尖锐阈值确定为$2$（拓扑维数的一半）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Engel群（一类非交换、幂零、非阿贝尔的Carnot群）上标准次拉普拉斯算子谱乘子有界性的尖锐正则性阈值。既往对称空间或Heisenberg群的谱乘子理论表明阈值常与拓扑维数相关，而本工作旨在将这一分析推进到结构更复杂的Engel群。

Method: 证明方法结合了两项关键技术：(1) 针对四次振子（quartic oscillators）的加权Plancherel估计；(2) 将谱空间分解为秩二子空间（rank-two spectral subspaces），从而在逃逸到无穷远时一致地控制参数导数。此外，对紧支撑于$(0,\infty)$的光滑乘子，作者计算了局部化核的主项及一阶修正。

Result: 在尺度不变局部$H^s$条件（$s>2$）下，$F(\mathcal L)$为弱$(1,1)$型且在所有$L^p\ (1<p<\infty)$上有界。结合已知的下界结果，尖锐阈值被确定为$2$，恰为拓扑维数的一半。局部$H^2$条件下弱$(1,1)$型估计是否成立仍为公开问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Engel群上次拉普拉斯算子谱乘子的尖锐$H^s$阈值（$s=2$），推广了谱乘子理论至更一般的Carnot群，并为局部$H^2$边界情形留下了待解决的开问题。

Abstract: We prove a spectral multiplier theorem for the standard sub-Laplacian on the Engel group under a scale-invariant local $H^s$ condition with $s>2$. The multiplier operators $F(\mathcal L)$ are of weak type $(1,1)$ and bounded on $L^p$ for every $1<p<\infty$. Together with the known lower bound, this identifies the sharp threshold as $2$, half the topological dimension. The proof combines a weighted Plancherel estimate for quartic oscillators with a decomposition into rank-two spectral subspaces, which controls parameter derivatives uniformly as one well escapes to infinity. For fixed smooth multipliers compactly supported in $(0,\infty)$, we also determine the leading term and first correction for the localized kernels. Whether the weak type $(1,1)$ estimate holds under the local $H^2$ condition remains open.

</details>


### [99] [Critical Sobolev Inequalities and Extremals for a Partially Fractional Grushin Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09001)
*S. Malhotra,L. Sharma,K. Sreenadh*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究部分分数阶 Grushin 型算子，建立各向异性 Poincaré 不等式、临界 Sobolev 不等式与集中紧性原理，并证明当 β>0 时集中只能发生在退化超平面上，从而得到最佳常数极值函数的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文关注一个耦合了 x 方向分数阶扩散与 y 方向退化局部扩散的部分分数阶 Grushin 型算子。由于 β>0 时权重 |x|^{2β} 破坏了 x 方向的平移不变性，经典的紧性方法难以直接使用，因此需要建立适配各向异性伸缩结构的分析框架，并刻画可能出现的集中现象。

Method: 采用各向异性伸缩 (x,y)↦(ρx,ρ^{α+β}y) 与相应的适配拟距离；在各向异性柱体上证明一致的分数阶 Grushin-Poincaré 不等式；构造基于二进柱体伸缩求和的 Riesz 型势，导出临界指数 2*_{α,β}=2Q_{α,β}/(Q_{α,β}-2α) 处的整体 Sobolev 不等式，其中 Q_{α,β}=n+(α+β)m；进而为相应能量空间建立集中紧性原理。

Result: 证明了各向异性柱体上的一致分数阶 Grushin-Poincaré 不等式；在尖锐临界 Sobolev 指数处建立了整体 Sobolev 不等式；建立了能量空间的集中紧性原理；并证明当 β>0 时集中被限制在退化超平面 Σ={(0,y): y∈R^m} 上。

Conclusion: 集中现象的局部化给出了最佳常数 S_{α,β}(n,m) 的极值函数的存在性，揭示了退化权重对紧性丧失位置的决定性作用。

Abstract: We study the partially fractional Grushin-type operator \begin{equation} \mathcal L_{α,β}u=(-Δ_x)^αu-(1+β)^2|x|^{2β}Δ_y u, \qquad (x,y)\in\mathbb{R}^n\times\mathbb{R}^m,\quad 0<α<1,\ β\ge 0, \end{equation} which couples fractional diffusion in $x$ with degenerate local diffusion in $y$. Working with the anisotropic dilations $(x,y)\mapsto(ρx,ρ^{α+β}y)$ and an adapted quasi-distance, we prove a uniform fractional Grushin-Poincaré inequality on anisotropic cylinders and, via a Riesz-type potential built from a telescoping sum over dyadic cylinders, a global Sobolev inequality at the sharp critical exponent $2^*_{α,β}=2Q_{α,β}/(Q_{α,β}-2α)$, where $Q_{α,β}=n+(α+β)m$. We then establish a concentration-compactness principle for the associated energy space. For $β>0$ the weight $|x|^{2β}$ destroys translation invariance in $x$, and we show that concentration is confined to the degenerate hyperplane $ Σ= \{(0,y):y\in\mathbb{R}^m\}$; this localization yields the existence of extremals for the sharp constant $S_{α,β}(n,m)$.

</details>


### [100] [Li-Yau and Harnack estimates for nonlinear parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09009)
*Thalia Jeffres,Xiaolong Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文为多类非线性抛物方程的正粘性解建立全局Harnack不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典线性热方程的全局Harnack理论与Li-Yau估计推广到非线性抛物情形。

Method: 利用半连续函数极大值原理，在两个独立时空点进行比较，并结合加权测地变分。

Result: 对正齐次一致抛物流、归一化加权p-Laplace热流及齐次各向同性扩散获全局Harnack不等式；归一化无穷-Laplace热流无需曲率假设，普通加权p-Laplace在非负Bakry-Émery Ricci下界下得到压力估计，并在一阶粘性意义下推出Li-Yau不等式。

Conclusion: 统一的比较方法给出多类非线性抛物方程的全局Harnack与Li-Yau型结果。

Abstract: We establish global Harnack inequalities for positive viscosity solutions of several classes of nonlinear parabolic equations. The equations include positively homogeneous uniformly parabolic flows on Euclidean space and flat tori, normalized weighted $p$-Laplacian heat flows, and homogeneous isotropic diffusions on closed smooth metric measure spaces. For the normalized infinity-Laplacian heat flow, no curvature assumption is needed. For the ordinary weighted $p$-Laplacian, we obtain pressure estimates under nonnegative Bakry-Émery Ricci lower bounds. The proofs combine comparisons at two independent space-time points using the maximum principle for semicontinuous functions with weighted geodesic variations. The corresponding Li-Yau inequalities then follow in the first-order viscosity sense.

</details>


### [101] [Sharp blow-up condition for a two-species cross attraction system in 3 or more dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09078)
*Suleyman Ulusoy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究 $d\geq3$ 维双物种双刺激退化趋化模型，在初始数据可积且二阶矩与能量有限条件下，证明自由能解（具自由能估计的弱解）对任意质量的局部存在性，并揭示解的整体存在与爆破行为由参数对的临界值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双物种双刺激退化趋化模型在 $d\geq3$ 下的适定性与解的大时间行为尚需刻画，特别是自由能解的存在性及决定整体存在或爆破的临界机制。

Method: 假设初始数据可积、有限二阶矩与能量，构造并分析自由能解（满足自由能估计的弱解）；通过识别方程系统中参数对的临界值，区分亚临界与超临界情形。

Result: 得到任意质量自由能解的局部存在性；参数对满足临界条件时存在整体能量解，违反临界条件时存在爆破的自由能解。

Conclusion: 解的整体存在与有限时间爆破由参数对的临界值决定，表明该退化趋化系统具有由参数控制的临界阈值现象。

Abstract: We consider a degenerate chemotaxis model with two-species and two-stimuli in dimension $d \geq 3$. Under the hypothesis of integrable initial data with finite second moment and energy, we show local-in-time existence for any mass of free-energy solutions, namely weak solutions with some free energy estimates. We exhibit that the qualitative behavior of solutions is decided by a set of critical values: there is a critical value of a parameter pair in the system of equations for which there is a global-in-time energy solution and there exist blowing-up free-energy solutions under a criticality condition is violated for the parameter pair.

</details>


### [102] [Fully Nonlinear Logistic Equations with Sanctuary Regions and Nonlocal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09271)
*Giulio Galise,Shuhei Kitano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有界域上含避难区（系数 k 可能消失）的全非线性非局部方程 Iu+μu=k(x)u^p 的正解，刻画了锐利存在区间并证明唯一性，同时分析了下端点与上端点（μ↑λ(Ω₀)）处解在两种不同尺度下的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当非局部一致椭圆算子的方程中系数 k 可在“避难区”Ω₀ 上消失时，正解的存在性区间、唯一性以及边界端点附近解的渐近形态，尤其是避难区导致的上端点爆破现象及其多尺度结构。

Method: 借助正主半特征值刻画锐利存在区间；证明解的唯一性；通过归一化轮廓分析下端点（解一致趋于零，归一化轮廓收敛到主特征函数）和上端点（μ↑λ(Ω₀) 时解在 Ω 内局部一致爆破），并用归一化得到两种尺度下的极限（分别在全空间 ℝⁿ 一致收敛和在 Ω\Ω₀ 局部一致收敛）。

Result: 得到锐利存在区间和唯一性；下端点归一化轮廓收敛到主特征函数；若 k 在避难区 Ω₀ 消失，则当 μ↑λ(Ω₀) 时解局部一致爆破，且 u_μ/‖u_μ‖_{L^∞(Ω)}→φ₀ 在 ℝⁿ 一致成立，u_μ/‖u_μ‖_{L^∞(Ω)}^{1/p}→(Iφ₀/k)^{1/p} 在 Ω\Ω₀ 局部一致成立。

Conclusion: 上端点爆破发生在两个不同尺度上，分别对应解的 L^∞ 归一化与次 L^∞ 归一化；避难区 Ω₀ 的主特征函数 φ₀ 控制整体轮廓，而 Iφ₀/k 控制避难区外的精细渐近。

Abstract: We study positive solutions of $Iu+μu=k(x)u^p$ in a bounded domain, where $p>1$, the nonnegative coefficient $k$ may vanish on a sanctuary region, and $I$ is a fully nonlinear nonlocal operator uniformly elliptic in the Caffarelli--Silvestre sense. We characterize the sharp existence interval in terms of positive principal half-eigenvalues and prove uniqueness. At the lower endpoint, the solutions vanish uniformly and their normalized profiles converge to the principal eigenfunction. If $k$ vanishes on a sanctuary $Ω_0$, then, as $μ\uparrowλ(Ω_0)$, the solutions blow up locally uniformly in $Ω$. Writing $φ_0=φ(\cdot,Ω_0)$ for the normalized positive principal eigenfunction in $Ω_0$, we prove \[ \frac{u_μ}{\|u_μ\|_{L^\infty(Ω)}}\toφ_0, \qquad \frac{u_μ}{\|u_μ\|_{L^\infty(Ω)}^{1/p}} \to\left(\frac{Iφ_0}{k}\right)^{1/p}, \] uniformly in $\mathbb{R}^n$ and locally uniformly in $Ω\setminus\overline{Ω_0}$, respectively. Thus the upper-endpoint blow-up occurs on two distinct scales.

</details>


### [103] [Neumann Schrödinger Estimates in Cylindrical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09378)
*Len Meas*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在半无限大圆柱域上研究半经典薛定谔方程的色散与Strichartz估计，边界为齐次Neumann条件。作者结合半经典微局部分析、振荡积分估计与适配Neumann反射的Airy–Poisson求和公式，得到局部时间色散估计及带导数损失的全局Strichartz估计，并应用于三次非线性薛定谔方程的局部适定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆柱域具有部分平坦边界，导致曲率在纵向退化并产生持续的glancing（掠射）相互作用，使该问题显著区别于严格凸域。同时Neumann边界条件在谱层面体现为Airy导数的零点而非Dirichlet对应的Airy零点，现有针对严格凸域或Dirichlet条件的色散/Strichartz理论无法直接覆盖这一情形，因此需要建立适配的线性框架。

Method: 采用半经典微局部分析与振荡积分估计；针对Neumann反射构造并运用Airy–Poisson求和公式来处理谱分布与边界层效应；利用薛定谔相函数的二次结构统一处理低频区域，并结合边界层分析导出全局估计。

Result: 建立了局部时间尖锐色散估计与全局Strichartz估计，得到局部化薛定谔传播子的均匀色散界；全局Strichartz估计带有导数损失 ρ(q)=3/2(1/2−1/q)。作为应用，证明了聚焦或散焦三次非线性薛定谔方程在H^s(Ω)（s>1）中的局部适定性。

Conclusion: 结果量化了部分平坦边界几何对薛定谔色散的影响，为相应的非线性动力学提供了尖锐的线性框架。

Abstract: We establish sharp local-in-time dispersive estimates and global Strichartz estimates for the semiclassical Schrödinger equation in a three-dimensional cylindrical domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{3}$ with homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions. The partially flat boundary leads to a degeneracy of curvature in the longitudinal direction and produces persistent glancing interactions that distinguish the cylindrical setting from strictly convex domains. At the spectral level, the Neumann condition is reflected in the appearance of the zeros of the Airy derivative in place of the Airy zeros associated with Dirichlet boundary conditions.
  Using semiclassical microlocal analysis, oscillatory integral estimates, and an Airy--Poisson summation formula adapted to Neumann reflection, we obtain uniform dispersive bounds for the localized Schrödinger propagator. The quadratic structure of the Schrödinger phase yields a uniform treatment of the low-frequency regime and, combined with the boundary-layer analysis, leads to global Strichartz estimates with derivative loss $$ ρ(q)=\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac12-\frac1q\right). $$ As an application, we prove local well-posedness in $H^{s}(Ω)$, $s>1$, for the cubic nonlinear Schrödinger equation with either focusing or defocusing sign and homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions. These results quantify the effect of partially flat boundary geometry on Schrödinger dispersion and provide a sharp linear framework for the corresponding nonlinear dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [Interior $C^2$ Estimates for 2-Sum Hessian Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09490)
*C. Y. Ren,X. L. Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 $\alpha\ge0$ 且 $D^2\alpha=\beta I$ 的假设下，于正分支 $\Delta u>0$ 上建立方程 $\sigma_2(D^2u)+\alpha(x)\sigma_1(D^2u)=f(x,u,Du)>0$ 解的内估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在为一类含一阶项系数的二阶 Hessian 型方程建立内部 $C^2$ 估计，克服系数 $\alpha$ 带来的困难，并厘清 $\alpha$ 有界是否足够这一自然问题。

Method: 通过引入一个额外变量并作四次（quartic）修正，将原方程提升到高一维空间中的二次（quadratic）Hessian 方程；由于涉及 $D^2u$ 的项相互抵消，可直接应用 Li--Wu 的内估计。同时对不依赖梯度的情形作了处理。

Result: 在 $\alpha\ge0$、$D^2\alpha=\beta I$ 条件下得到内部 $C^2$ 估计；梯度无关情形亦得到处理；并给出一个例子说明仅有 $\alpha$ 的 $L^\infty$ 界不足以得到相应估计。

Conclusion: 对系数 $\alpha$ 施加 $D^2\alpha=\beta I$ 的结构性假设可使问题归约为高维二次 Hessian 方程并获得内估计，而单纯 $L^\infty$ 有界性不充分。

Abstract: This paper establishes an interior $C^2$ estimate for solutions of \[ σ_2(D^2u)+α(x)σ_1(D^2u)=f(x,u,Du)>0, \] on the positive branch $Δu>0$, under the assumptions $α\ge0$ and $D^2α=βI$. Using one extra variable and a quartic correction, the equation is reduced to a quadratic Hessian equation in one higher dimension. The terms involving $D^2u$ cancel, so the interior estimate of Li--Wu \cite{LW} applies. The gradient-independent case is also treated. Finally, an example shows that an $L^\infty$ bound on $α$ alone is not enough.

</details>


### [105] [Sharp Hilbert transform norms on Schatten classes: classification and optimal spectral corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10158)
*Zhouyu Long*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Hilbert变换在$L^p(\mathbb{R};\mathcal{S}_p)$上及有序Hilbert Schur乘子在$\mathcal{S}_p$上的范数恰为$C_p=\cot(\pi/(2p^*))$，其中$p^*=\max\{p,p/(p-1)\}$，从而解决了Gohberg-Krupnik sharp Volterra猜想，并同时解决了Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想与Laeng猜想5.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在确立Hilbert变换与Schur乘子在非交换$L^p$空间上的精确完全有界范数，验证长期未决的Gohberg-Krupnik sharp Volterra猜想，并统一处理相关的Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想与Laeng猜想。

Method: 证明基于Heinävaara的迹联合谱测度，使用含两个非负余项的谱缺陷恒等式；对有限带号实岭和进行分类，确定最小齐次超调和优函数作为逐点最小加性特征值修正，并用有限幂零矩阵的加权角测度的弱-*极限刻画相关角锥。

Result: Hilbert变换与有序Hilbert Schur乘子的范数均为$C_p=\cot(\pi/(2p^*))$，标量Hilbert变换与Schur乘子具有相同的典范完全有界范数；证明同时给出有限维实现，解决Riesz-Titchmarsh猜想与Laeng猜想5.7对所有移位的情形，并推广到具$\ell^p$直和值域的实Hilbert变换束有限族。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了Gohberg-Krupnik sharp Volterra猜想及相关猜想，建立了非交换$L^p$上Hilbert变换范数的精确公式，并提供了基于谱缺陷恒等式与角锥刻画的统一分析框架。

Abstract: For every $1<p<\infty$, the Hilbert transform on $L^p(\mathbb{R};\mathcal{S}_p)$ and the ordered Hilbert Schur multiplier on $\mathcal{S}_p$ have norm $C_p=\cot(π/(2p^*))$, where $p^*=\max\{p,p/(p-1)\}$. The scalar Hilbert transform and the Schur multiplier have the same canonical completely bounded norm. This proves the Gohberg-Krupnik sharp Volterra conjecture. A simultaneous finite-dimensional realization also resolves the Riesz-Titchmarsh conjecture and Laeng's Conjecture 5.7 for all shifts. The norm identities extend to finite families of real Hilbert-transform pencils with $\ell^p$ direct-sum ranges.
  The proof uses a spectral-defect identity with two nonnegative remainders, based on Heinävaara's tracial joint spectral measure. It classifies trace inequalities for finite signed real ridge sums and identifies the least homogeneous superharmonic majorant as the pointwise least additive eigenvalue correction. The associated angular cone is characterized by nonnegative potentials and realized by weak-* limits of weighted angular measures of finite nilpotent matrices.

</details>


### [106] [Logarithmic Hessian estimates for non-uniformly elliptic equations in non-divergence form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09494)
*Jongmyeong Kim,Se-Chan Lee*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文建立了非一致椭圆型散度型方程解的一致对数Hessian估计，构造反例说明幂型估计不成立、对数指数在平面内最优，并推广至强解及Grushin型方程的正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非一致椭圆方程中，Hessian的一致估计通常只能得到幂型（如Hölder）模估计，但在退化程度较高时幂型一致估计可能失效，需要确定在何种模下解仍然满足一致Hessian估计及其最优指数。

Method: 证明基于凸分析给出的测度估计，无需局部化或覆盖论证；同时构造反例排除一致幂型Hessian估计，据此论证对数指数在平面情形的尖锐性。

Result: 得到非一致椭圆型方程解的统一对数Hessian估计；反例表明一致幂型Hessian估计不成立，且所给对数指数在平面内是尖锐的；估计可推广到强解，并用于导出Grushin型方程的正则性结果。

Conclusion: 非一致椭圆方程解在Hessian层面满足一致对数模估计，该模是最优的，且结果可延伸至强解与Grushin型方程的正则性分析。

Abstract: We establish uniform logarithmic Hessian estimates for solutions of non-uniformly elliptic equations in non-divergence form. The proof is based on a measure estimate from convex analysis without localization or covering arguments. Moreover, we construct a counterexample that rules out uniform power-type Hessian estimates and shows the sharpness of the logarithmic exponent in the plane. Finally, we extend our estimates to strong solutions and apply them to obtain regularity results for Grushin-type equations.

</details>


### [107] [Collapse and saturation for radial nodal solutions of a Lorentz--Minkowski mean curvature equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10166)
*Alberto Boscaggin,Francesca Colasuonno,Ricardo Ziegele*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究洛伦兹-闵可夫斯基平均曲率算子在径向球上的Dirichlet问题，证明解在μ→∞时的先验二分性，并在适当指定零点数时实现两种渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画洛伦兹-闵可夫斯基平均曲率方程解在μ→+∞时的渐近行为，需要理解带指定零点数的解族是收敛到零还是收敛到分段仿射极限函数，并验证两种可能是否都实际出现。

Method: 在径向设定下研究Dirichlet问题；对具有指定零点数的解证明μ→+∞的渐近二分性；借助已有文献的乘性结果，细化打靶构造（shooting construction）得到收敛到零的小解族，并通过反向打靶论证得到收敛到饱和分段仿射剖面的解族。

Result: 证明了解在μ→+∞时的先验二分性：要么一致收敛到0，要么收敛到满足|u∞'|=1几乎处处、且具有相同节点性质的分段仿射极限函数u∞；当零点数适当指定时，两种情形均可实现——存在收敛到零的小解族与收敛到饱和分段仿射剖面的解族。

Conclusion: 带指定零点数的解族在μ→+∞时呈现二分渐近结构，且两种替代均可由打靶与反向打靶构造具体实现，推广并细化了洛伦兹-闵可夫斯基平均曲率方程的乘性与渐近性结果。

Abstract: We consider the Dirichlet problem for the Lorentz--Minkowski mean curvature operator in a radial setting, $$ -\operatorname{div}\left(\frac{\nabla u}{\sqrt{1-|\nabla u|^2}}\right) = λu + μg(u) \quad \text{in } \mathcal B_R, \qquad u = 0 \quad \text{on } \partial\mathcal B_R, $$ on a ball of radius $R$ centered at the origin $\mathcal B_R \subset \mathbb{R}^N$, where $λ, μ\geq 0$ are parameters, and the nonlinearity $g$ satisfies suitable growth assumptions at zero. We first prove an a priori dichotomy for the asymptotic behaviour, as $μ\to +\infty$, of solutions with a prescribed number of zeros: either they converge uniformly to $0$, or they converge to a piecewise affine limit function $u_\infty$, with $|u_\infty'|=1$ a.e., having the same nodal properties. We then show that, when the number of zeros is appropriately prescribed, both alternatives are actually realized. More precisely, building on the multiplicity result in [A. Boscaggin, F. Colasuonno, and R. Ziegele, \emph{Positive and nodal solutions for the Minkowski mean curvature equation: multiplicity and asymptotics}, arXiv:2607.15956 (2026)], we refine the shooting construction therein to obtain a family of small solutions converging to zero and, through a backward shooting argument, a family of large solutions converging to a saturated piecewise affine profile.

</details>


### [108] [New result on Lane-Emden conjecture with exponents in a disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09526)
*Haoyang Lu,Zhitao Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究Lane-Emden系统在特定$(p,q)$圆盘区域内的Liouville定理，证明了当圆盘与临界双曲线相切且不施加增长或衰减假设时，系统不存在正经典解，除非$p=q=(N+2)/(N-2)$；对$N\ge5$，该圆盘包含未被既有准则覆盖的开集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lane-Emden猜想是椭圆型偏微分方程领域的核心问题之一。已有Liouville型准则（如Busca-Manásevich、Souplet、Li-Li-Wei等）给出的非存在性区域尚未覆盖全部临界范围，作者希望扩展可用准则覆盖的$(p,q)$参数区域，在不假设解增长或衰减条件的前提下获得新的Liouville定理。

Method: 首先利用已有Liouville准则缩减指数范围；随后构造向量场，推导散度不等式；再综合运用Sobolev嵌入定理、Gagliardo-Nirenberg插值不等式与Young不等式，对任意正解$(u,v)$建立带合适权重的局部一致积分估计$\int_{B_1}(u^rv^{2p}+u^{2q}v^s)dx\le C(N,p,q)$；最后通过伸缩变换得到正经典解的非存在性。
