Result: 在$N\ge3$、$p,q>0$、$pq>1$且满足$\left(p-\frac{2}{N-2}\right)^2+\left(q-\frac{2}{N-2}\right)^2\le\frac{2N^2}{(N-2)^2}$的条件下，系统在圆盘内无正经典解，除非$p=q=(N+2)/(N-2)$。对$N\ge5$，该圆盘含有一个非空开集，不被Busca-Manásevich、Souplet及Li-Li-Wei的显式条件所覆盖。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Lane-Emden系统Liouville定理的适用范围，所给圆盘与临界双曲线相切，且无需增长或衰减假设，填补了既有准则未能覆盖的参数区域，为Lane-Emden猜想的进一步研究提供了新工具与结果。

Abstract: We obtain new results on Lane-Emden conjecture to prove a Liouville theorem for the Lane-Emden system $$\begin{cases} -Δu=v^p,& x\in \mathbb{R}^N,\\ -Δv=u^q,& x\in \mathbb{R}^N,\end{cases}$$ with $N\ge3$, $p,q>0$, $pq>1$, and \[ \left(p-\frac2{N-2}\right)^2+ \left(q-\frac2{N-2}\right)^2\le\frac{2N^2}{(N-2)^2}, \] the system has no positive classical solution as $(p,q)$ in the disk unless $p=q=(N+2)/(N-2)$, no growth or decay assumptions are imposed, and the disk is tangent to the critical hyperbola. Especially, for $N\ge5$, the disk contains a nonempty open set not covered by the criteria of Busca-Manásevich~\cite{BM} and Souplet~\cite{Souplet}, or by the explicit conditions of Li-Li-Wei~\cite{LLW}. After reducing the exponent range by known Liouville criteria, we first construct vector fields to derive a divergence inequality, then apply Sobolev embedding theorem, Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, and Young's inequality to give a uniform local integral bound $\int_{B_1}(u^rv^{2p}+u^{2q}v^s)dx\le C(N,p,q)$ for suitable weights for any positive solution $(u,v)$, finally the nonexistence of positive classical solutions follows by scaling.

</details>


### [109] [Quantitative thermoviscous Darcy limits in periodic and bounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09528)
*Jiaojiao Pan,Luqi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了三维非均匀不可压缩流体在温度依赖黏性与准静态热反馈下的定量Darcy极限，给出误差界并构造微观弱解与局部强有效解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带温度依赖黏性和准静态热反馈的三维非均匀不可压缩流体，在含小孔介质中如何由微观模型严格推导出宏观Darcy型有效模型，并获得定量收敛速率。

Method: 孔洞尺度为ε^α、间距为ε（1&lt;α&lt;3）；归一化前流体与固体热导率为σ_ε^2κ_f与σ_ε^2κ_s，σ_ε^2∼ε^{3−α}以在极限中保留热反馈。采用界面无关的温度估计控制黏性差异，用加权Stokes残差保留系数与元胞压力交换子；有界情形使用螺线管壁面修正与逐胞散度修复，并构造固定参数微观弱解。

Result: 对充分正则的参考解及显式吸收条件下，平方密度、速度与温度误差被加权初始偏差及ε^{α−1}+ε^{3−α}（环面）或ε^{α−1}+ε^{(3−α)/2}（有界域）控制。

Conclusion: 在环面与C^{3,β}有界域两种设定下均得到定量Darcy极限；局部强有效解需额外收缩条件，有界域还需更强边界正则性。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish quantitative Darcy limits for a three-dimensional inhomogeneous incompressible fluid with temperature-dependent viscosity and quasi-static thermal feedback. The domain is either the flat torus or a bounded domain with $C^{3,β}$ boundary, $0<β<1$. The holes have size $\varepsilon^α$ and separation of order $\varepsilon$, with $1<α<3$. Before normalization, the fluid and solid conductivities are $σ_\varepsilon^2κ_f$ and $σ_\varepsilon^2κ_s$, where $σ_\varepsilon^2\sim\varepsilon^{3-α}$. This scaling preserves the thermal feedback in the limit. For a sufficiently regular reference solution and under an explicit absorption condition, the squared density, velocity and temperature errors are bounded by the weighted initial discrepancy together with $\varepsilon^{α-1}+\varepsilon^{3-α}$ on the torus and $\varepsilon^{α-1}+\varepsilon^{(3-α)/2}$ in a bounded domain. An interface-independent temperature estimate controls the viscosity discrepancy, and a weighted Stokes residual retains the coefficient and cell-pressure commutators. The bounded comparison uses a solenoidal wall correction and cellwise divergence repair. We also construct fixed-parameter microscopic weak solutions. The local strong effective solutions require a separate contraction condition in both settings and stronger boundary regularity in the bounded case.

</details>


### [110] [Shortest Paths in Domains with Crack-Induced Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09540)
*Phiet Dau The,Phan Thanh An*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将含裂纹域中的最短路径问题表述为带状态约束的Hamilton–Jacobi方程，建立了比较原理与值函数唯一性，并给出裂纹上滑动/照射两区域的几何刻画及裂尖衍射律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Crandall–Ishii–Lions加倍技术在裂纹处失效，需要新的论证工具来处理这一低维状态约束下的最短路径/粘性解问题。

Method: 将裂纹视为双侧屏障与低维状态约束（沿两唇分别承载），裂纹粘合部分上约束呈变分不等式形式；采用基于轨线的论证替代经典加倍技术证明比较原理与唯一性；通过最优性条件推导自由边界、滑动与照射区、掠射接触、无反射及裂尖衍射律。

Result: 完整刻画了滑动区与照射区两类区域，包含切向（掠射）接触、正则裂纹点处无反射、裂尖衍射律；在可精确求解构型上的正向数值模拟复现了三种区域与裂尖衍射律，精度与闭式解一致。

Conclusion: 含裂纹最短路径可由带状态约束的Hamilton–Jacobi框架统一描述，其自由边界结构及传播规律均可纯粹由最优性导出，理论刻画与数值结果一致。

Abstract: We formulate the shortest-path problem in a domain containing a crack as a Hamilton--Jacobi equation carrying a state constraint on the crack, which on the binding part of the crack takes the form of a variational inequality: the crack is a two-sided barrier that may be touched and followed but not traversed, so that it acts as a lower-dimensional state constraint, carried separately by each of its two lips, which admits tangential propagation but forbids crossing. We establish a comparison principle and uniqueness of the value function through trajectory-based arguments that circumvent the failure of the classical doubling technique of Crandall, Ishii, and Lions at the crack. The variational inequality induces a free boundary on the crack separating a sliding regime, where optimal trajectories run along the crack over a finite segment, from an illuminated regime, where the crack is reached transversally by direct rays; we give a complete geometric characterization of both regimes, including grazing (tangential) contact, the absence of reflection at regular crack points, and a diffraction law at crack tips, all derived from optimality alone. A simulation of the forward problem on an exactly solvable configuration reproduces the three regimes and the tip-diffraction law to the accuracy of the closed-form solution.

</details>


### [111] [Faber-Krahn inequality for the first Dirichlet eigenvalues of the logarithmic Hardy operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09578)
*Ying Wang,Tao Zhang,Wanwan Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了含对数 Hardy 势的对数 Hardy 算子 $\mathcal{L}_\mu=(-\Delta)^{\ln}+2\mu\log\frac1{|x|}$ 在含原点有界 Lipschitz 域上的 Dirichlet 特征值 Faber--Krahn 不等式，证明 $\mu\in(-1,0)$ 时球是最小化一特征值的唯一极值域，并说明 $\mu>0$ 时结论失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画带对数 Hardy 势的非局部算子 $\mathcal{L}_\mu$ 的谱几何性质，尤其是经典 Faber--Krahn 型最优等周不等式是否成立，以及 Hardy 势符号对极值域的影响。

Method: 研究含原点有界 Lipschitz 域 $\Omega\subset\mathbb{R}^N$ 上算子 $\mathcal{L}_\mu=(-\Delta)^{\ln}+2\mu\log(1/|x|)$ 的 Dirichlet 特征值 $\{\lambda^{\ln,\mu}_k(\Omega)\}_{k\in\mathbb N}$；通过建立 $\mu\in(-1,0)$ 的 Faber--Krahn 不等式并对 $\mu>0$ 构造双球加管道的反例。

Result: $\mu\in(-1,0)$ 时，固定体积下原点为中心的球最小化第一特征值，且等号仅对该球成立；$\mu>0$ 时该不等式失效，由显式双球加管道结构给出反例。

Conclusion: 受对数 Hardy 势符号控制，Faber--Krahn 不等式在 $\mu<0$ 时成立并以球为唯一极小元，而在 $\mu>0$ 时不再成立，揭示了势与算子谱最优形状之间的临界差异。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is to study Faber--Krahn inequality of
  Dirichlet eigenvalues $\{ λ^{\ln,μ}_{k}(Ω)\}_{k\in \N}$
  for the logarithmic Hardy operator \[ \mathcal{L}_μ=(-Δ)^{\ln}+2μ\log\frac{1}{|x|} \] on a bounded Lipschitz domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^{N}$ containing the origin, with $μ>-1$. We establish a Faber--Krahn inequality for $μ\in(-1,0)$, the ball centered at the origin minimizes the first eigenvalue at fixed volume, with equality only for that ball. Finally, we show this inequality fails for $μ>0$ via an explicit two-ball-and-tube construction.

</details>


### [112] [Regularity Estimates for Quasilinear Elliptic Equations with Measure Data and Matrix-Valued Weight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09594)
*Sun-Sig Byun,Xiatong Li,Yeonghun Youn*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带有限Radon测度与退化矩阵权 $\mathbb{M}$ 的拟线性椭圆Dirichlet问题，在 $\mathbb{M}$ 满足小log-BMO条件下证明SOLA存在性，并建立 $|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M}Du$ 的局部Calderón-Zygmund估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带测度数据的拟线性椭圆问题中，退化矩阵权会破坏经典椭圆理论的正则性框架，需要在小log-BMO假设下寻求解的存在性及梯度正则性刻画。

Method: 对 $\mathbb{M}$ 施加小log-BMO条件，先构造逼近解列并取极限得到SOLA；再利用分数次极大函数 $\mathcal{M}_1(\mu)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}$ 作为控制量，建立加权梯度的局部Calderón-Zygmund型估计。

Result: 证明了该Dirichlet问题SOLA的存在性，并得到 $|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M}Du$ 由 $\mathcal{M}_1(\mu)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}$ 控制的局部Calderón-Zygmund估计。

Conclusion: 在退化矩阵权的小log-BMO框架下，测度数据问题的解存在且加权梯度具有由分数次极大函数刻画的局部正则性。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the existence and local gradient regularity of solutions to a quasilinear elliptic Dirichlet problem with finite Radon measure and a degenerate matrix-valued weight. Under a small log-BMO condition on $\mathbb M$, we first prove the existence of a SOLA (solution obtained by a limit of approximations) and then establish a local Calderón-Zygmund estimate for $|\mathbb{M}|^{\frac{1}{p-1}}\mathbb{M} Du$ in terms of the fractional maximal function $\mathcal{M}_1(μ)^{\frac{1}{p-1}}$.

</details>


### [113] [Stability of optimal transport on sub-Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09688)
*Bang-Xian Han,Zhuo-Nan Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究平方Carnot–Carathéodory距离下最优输运的稳定性，得到了在完备Riemann与fat次Riemann流形上尖锐的W_1^{1/4}输运映射稳定性估计，以及在John区域上Kantorovich势的对数L^2稳定性。对于势，在二生成结构上获得任意Hölder指数0<α<1/2，在Riemann、fat情形及所有二步Carnot群上得到1/2；三原子例子表明映射与势关于W_1的指数分别不能超过1/4与1/2。映射估计源于对偶缺陷的强制性，势估计源于Kantorovich泛函的定量凹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究平方Carnot–Carathéodory距离下最优输运的稳定性，刻画输运映射与Kantorovich势在源测度扰动下（以W_1度量）的定量连续性，并确定其中最优的Hölder/对数指数。

Method: 在完备Riemann与fat次Riemann流形上，假设源支撑有界及密度上界，证明尖锐的W_1^{1/4}映射稳定性估计；在John区域且密度有双侧界时，对源附近等正则可微的完备光滑次Riemann流形及任意紧目标，证明Kantorovich势的对数L^2稳定性。技术层面：映射估计基于对偶缺陷的强制性，势估计基于Kantorovich泛函的定量凹性。并用三原子构造与逐步等正则区域内构造光滑输运族来验证最优性。

Result: 得到尖锐的W_1^{1/4}映射稳定性估计；势的对数L^2稳定性；在二生成结构上势具有任意Hölder指数0<α<1/2，在Riemann、fat情形及所有二步Carnot群上达到1/2。三原子例子显示映射与势关于W_1的指数上限分别为1/4与1/2（在水平秩至少为2的任何光滑次Riemann流形上）。还构造出在步数s的等正则区域中，W_1中阶为t的目标变差导致映射变差阶为t^{1/s}的光滑输运族。

Conclusion: 平方Carnot–Carathéodory距离下最优输运的映射与势稳定性指数在其相应设定中是尖锐的：映射指数不能超过1/4，势指数不能超过1/2；等正则区域内映射变差随目标W_1变差呈t^{1/s}的幂律，反映步数s的影响。

Abstract: We study the stability of optimal transport for the squared Carnot--Carathéodory distance. We prove the sharp $W_1^{1/4}$ stability estimate for optimal transport maps on complete Riemannian and fat sub-Riemannian manifolds, assuming bounded source support and an upper density bound. On John domains with two-sided density bounds, we obtain logarithmic $L^2$ stability of Kantorovich potentials on complete smooth sub-Riemannian manifolds that are equiregular near the source, with arbitrary compact targets. For potentials, we obtain every Hölder exponent $0<α<1/2$ on two-generating structures, and the exponent $1/2$ in the Riemannian and fat settings and on all step-two Carnot groups. Three-atom examples show that the map and potential exponents with respect to $W_1$ cannot exceed $1/4$ and $1/2$, respectively, on any smooth sub-Riemannian manifold of horizontal rank at least two. We also construct smooth transport families whose target variation of order $t$ in $W_1$ produces map variation of order $t^{1/s}$ in an equiregular region of step $s$. The map estimates follow from coercivity of the duality defect, while the potential estimates follow from quantitative concavity of the Kantorovich functional.

</details>


### [114] [Strichartz estimates on two-dimensional irrational tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09689)
*Ming Wang,Yuanjia Xiong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了 $\mathbb T^2$ 上色散关系 $n_1^2+\alpha n_2^2$（任意 $\alpha>0$）的 $L^4$ Strichartz 估计，损失由归一化平方 Fourier 系数的 Shannon 熵及其支撑基数 $S$ 控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Herr 与 Kwak 此前给出基数估计（有理色散情形），Bourgain 与 Demeter 在频率盒上仅有 $N^\varepsilon$ 损失。本文旨在为无理色散提供对应的基数型估计，并改进损失阶。

Method: 结合加权关联（incidence）估计、光滑局部化与熵递归（entropy recursion）三种技术。

Result: 在固定时间区间上得到 $(\log\#S)^{1/2}$ 的范数损失，常数对 $\alpha$ 局部一致；在频率盒上将 Bourgain–Demeter 的 $N^\varepsilon$ 损失改进为 $(\log N)^{1/2}$。

Conclusion: 建立了无理色散情形下与 Herr–Kwak 基数估计相对应的 $L^4$ Strichartz 估计，并给出熵控损失与频率盒上对数级损失的改进。

Abstract: We prove an $L^4$ Strichartz estimate for the dispersion $n_1^2+αn_2^2$ on $\mathbb T^2$, for every $α>0$. The loss is controlled by the Shannon entropy of the normalized squared Fourier coefficients and the cardinality of their support $S$. In particular, we obtain a norm loss of $(\log\#S)^{1/2}$ on fixed time intervals, with constants locally uniform in $α$. This provides an irrational-dispersion counterpart of the cardinality estimate of Herr and Kwak [Forum Math. Pi (2024)] and improves the $N^\varepsilon$ loss of Bourgain and Demeter [Ann. of Math. (2015)] to $(\log N)^{1/2}$ on frequency boxes. The proof combines weighted incidence estimates, smooth localization, and an entropy recursion.

</details>


### [115] [Carleman Estimates for Critical Electromagnetic Schrödinger Operators on Conic Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09721)
*Baiyang Yin,Fang Zhang,Junyong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 建立了锥奇异空间上带临界电磁奇异位势的薛定谔算子的L^p-L^q型Carleman估计，并借此证明唯一延拓性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在锥奇异空间上，带尺度临界电磁奇异位势的薛定谔算子的Carleman型加权估计及唯一延拓性此前尚缺乏结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在乘积锥X=C(Y)=(0,∞)×Y（度规g=dr^2+r^2h，维数d≥3）上，对薛定谔算子H_{A,V}（含尺度临界电磁奇异位势）证明L^p-L^q型Carleman估计，采用二次权函数φ(r)=(1/4)(log r)^2，并保持对参数τ一致。

Result: 得到估计 ||e^{τφ}u||_{L^{2d/(d-2)}(X)} ≤ C ||e^{τφ}H_{A,V}u||_{L^{2d/(d+2)}(X)}，对τ一致成立。

Conclusion: 作为Carleman估计的应用，当横截面Y=S^{d-1}时，证明了该电磁薛定谔算子的唯一延拓性。

Abstract: We establish an $L^p$--$L^q$-type Carleman estimate for the Schrödinger operator $H_{A,V}$ with scaling-critical electromagnetic singular potentials on a conical singular space $(X,g)$ of dimension $d\geq3$, where the metric is $g=dr^2+r^2h$ and $X=C(Y)=(0,\infty)_r\times Y$ is a product cone over the closed Riemannian manifold $(Y,h)$. More precisely, we prove $$ \|e^{τφ}u\|_{L^{2d/(d-2)}(X)}\le C\,\|e^{τφ}H_{A,V}u\|_{L^{2d/(d+2)}(X)} $$ with the quadratic weight $φ(r)=\tfrac14(\log r)^2$, uniformly in $τ$. As an application of this Carleman estimate, we prove the unique continuation property for this electromagnetic Schrödinger operator when the cross-section is $Y=\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$.

</details>


### [116] [Rigidity of stationary black holes with small ADM angular momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09729)
*Alexandru F. Radu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：在整体正则性、双分支结构与极大超曲面假设下，ADM 角动量足够小的光滑渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞必为 Kerr 黑洞，阈值依赖固定的几何界；核心是建立任意角动量下的“无穷远处 Kerr 刚性”，从而回答 Alexakis–Ionescu–Klainerman 的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alexakis、Ionescu 与 Klainerman 此前的结果需要在双分支球面上对稳态场施加小性假设。本文希望将该假设替换为无穷远处（ADM）角动量的小性，从而在更自然的物理量条件下建立整体刚性，并处理任意角动量的无穷远刚性这一关键障碍。

Method: 将真空度量变换为一个 Einstein–Maxwell 族；沿该族连续延拓构造紧致极大超曲面，并比较其限制端处的零测地聚焦与紧致极大超曲面上的面积–电荷不等式；等号成立同时确定变换后的度量及其稳态场，再通过逆变换恢复 Kerr 度量；最后利用两个 Kerr 恒等式得到视界估计，用于整体结论。

Result: 在整体正则性、双分支与极大超曲面假设下，只要 ADM 角动量足够小（阈值由固定几何界决定），渐近平坦稳态真空黑洞即为 Kerr 黑洞；并建立了任意角动量情形下无穷远处的 Kerr 刚性。

Conclusion: 该工作把小性条件从双分支处的稳态场转移到无穷远处的角动量，给出了 Alexakis–Ionescu–Klainerman 问题的肯定回答；其关键机制是 Einstein–Maxwell 族上的面积–电荷不等式与 Kerr 恒等式，等号情形刻画了 Kerr 度量并导出所需视界估计。

Abstract: We prove global rigidity of smooth asymptotically flat stationary vacuum black holes with sufficiently small ADM angular momentum, under global regularity, bifurcation, and maximal-hypersurface assumptions, with a threshold depending on fixed geometric bounds. This answers the question of Alexakis, Ionescu, and Klainerman concerning replacement of their smallness assumption on the stationary field at the bifurcation sphere by smallness of the angular momentum at infinity. The main step establishes Kerr rigidity near infinity for arbitrary angular momentum. We transform the vacuum metric into an Einstein--Maxwell family and compare null focusing at its limiting end with the area--charge inequality on compact maximal hypersurfaces, constructed by continuation through the family. Equality determines both the transformed metric and its stationary field, from which the inverse transformation recovers Kerr. Two Kerr identities then give the horizon estimate required for the global conclusion.

</details>


### [117] [Strong transport noise prevents self-intersection in 2D fluid-structure interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09811)
*Dominic Breit,Prince Romeo Mensah*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究二维粘性不可压缩流体与边界上柔性弹性壳的相互作用，壳由完美弹性线性化梁方程建模。作者证明，在结构方程中加入物理上合理且足够强的输运噪声后，自相交在任何给定终止时刻之前以任意小于1的给定概率不发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体-结构相互作用中，即使对完美弹性板这一理想化情形，能否长期排除结构自相交仍是公开问题。作者希望借助噪声扰动来规避这一困难。

Method: 在二维域中考虑粘性不可压缩流体与边界柔性弹性壳的耦合系统，壳采用完美弹性线性化梁方程建模；在结构方程中引入物理上合理且足够强的输运噪声（transport noise），并进行随机分析。

Result: 证明加入足够强的输运噪声后，结构自相交在任意给定终止时刻之前不会发生，且该结论以任意小于1的指定概率成立。

Conclusion: 物理上合理的输运噪声可有效排除弹性壳的长期自相交，从而为该流体-结构相互作用的公开问题提供了一种随机化解决途径。

Abstract: We consider the interaction of a viscous incompressible fluid with a flexible elastic shell occupying the boundary of the fluid's domain in two space dimensions. The shell is modelled by a perfectly elastic linearised beam equation. It is an open problem in fluid-structure interaction, even for the idealised case of a perfectly elastic plate, if a self-intersection of the structure can be excluded in the long run. We show that by adding a physically well-motived and sufficiently strong transport noise in the structure equation, the self-intersection does not occur before any given end-time with any prescribed probability smaller than 1.

</details>


### [118] [Self-similar solutions to the steady Navier-Stokes equations in arbitrarily high dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09840)
*Changfeng Gui,Hao Liu,Yun Wang,Chunjing Xie*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究带齐次力的稳态Navier-Stokes方程的自相似解，去除维数限制，证明所有高维情形下(-1)-齐次解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作仅在4≤n≤16维、无小性假设下建立了(-1)-齐次解的存在性，存在维数限制。本文旨在去除该限制，将存在性结果推广到所有更高维数。

Method: 引入新要素：对球面上总水头压力建立与维数无关的先验估计，通过将源-通量方法及Wang-Yang压力加权恒等式适配到齐次框架得到；并结合先前工作中径向速度与总水头压力的结构关系。

Result: 在带(-3)-齐次局部Lipschitz力的稳态Navier-Stokes方程中，去除了维数限制，在所有高维情形下获得了自相似解的存在性，闭合了所有维数下的先验估计。

Conclusion: 通过维数无关的压力先验估计，成功将(-1)-齐次自相似解的存在性结果推广至任意高维，去除了此前的16维上限。

Abstract: We study $(-1)$-homogeneous solutions of the steady Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb R^n\setminus\{0\}$ with $(-3)$-homogeneous, locally Lipschitz forces. Previous work [2] established the existence of $(-1)$-homogeneous solutions without any smallness assumption in dimensions $4\leq n\leq16$. In this paper, we remove the dimension restriction and obtain the existence of self-similar solutions in all higher dimensions. The new ingredient is a dimension-independent a priori estimate for the total head pressure on the sphere, obtained by adapting the source-flux method and the weighted identity of the pressure of Wang and Yang [28] to the homogeneous setting. This combines with the structural relation between the radial velocity and the total head pressure used in [2] to close the a priori estimates for the solutions in all dimensions.

</details>


### [119] [Observable sets for the Schrödinger equation with potential $\lvert x\rvert$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09850)
*Lu Chen,Haolin Liu,Hongyu Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：对一维薛定谔方程 $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$，可测集在每个正时刻可观测当且仅当它是 thick 的，且固定观测时间下可观测常数仅依赖 thickness 参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Gengsheng Wang 关于该非谐振子模型中 thick 集与可观测集关系的公开问题。

Method: 研究 $L^2(\mathbb R)$ 上与算子 $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$ 相关联的一维薛定谔方程的可观测集，通过建立可测集的 thick 性质与可观测性之间的等价刻画，并分析观测常数对 thickness 参数的一致依赖性。

Result: 可测集在每个正时刻可观测当且仅当它是 thick 的；对每个固定观测时间，可观测常数在所有具有给定 thickness 参数的可测集上一致有界。

Conclusion: 该定理完整刻画了 thick 集与可观测集的等价关系，解决了 Gengsheng Wang 针对该非谐振子提出的问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we study observable sets for the one-dimensional Schrödinger equation associated with the operator $H=-d^2/dx^2+|x|$ on $L^2(\mathbb R)$. We prove that a measurable set is observable at every positive time if and only if it is thick. For each fixed observation time, the observability constant is uniform over all measurable sets with prescribed thickness parameters. The theorem answers the question concerning the relationship between thick and observable set raised by Gengsheng Wang [12] for this anharmonic oscillator.

</details>


### [120] [Nonnegative Viscosity and Weak Solutions to Trudinger's Parabolic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09905)
*Peter Lindqvist,Mikko Parviainen,Jarkko Siltakoski*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了方程 ∂_t(|u|^{p-2}u)=div(|∇u|^{p-2}∇u) 的粘性解与弱解的关系，证明对非负函数二者等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性解（viscosity solutions）与弱解（weak solutions）是偏微分方程的两套核心解概念，其定义机制不同：前者基于极值原理与测试函数的不等式约束，后者基于积分恒等式与分布意义。对退化抛物型方程而言，二者的等价性尚不清楚，因此作者意在厘清这两类解在该方程下的关系。

Method: 针对含 p-拉普拉斯算子（p-Laplacian）的退化抛物型方程 ∂_t(|u|^{p-2}u)=div(|∇ u|^{p-2}∇ u)，通过比较粘性解与弱解的定义，构造相互验证的推理，重点处理非负函数情形下的正则性与比较原理，以证明两类解互相蕴含。

Result: 证明了对非负函数而言，该方程的粘性解与弱解是彼此等价的解概念（至少在该限制条件下成立）。

Conclusion: 在该退化抛物型方程中，非负情形下粘性解与弱解可相互转化与统一，为后续利用粘性解方法研究此类方程的解的存在性、唯一性与正则性提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the relation between viscosity solutions and weak solutions of the equation $$\partial_t\left(|u|^{p-2}u\right)\,=\, \operatorname{div}(|\nabla u|^{p-2}\nabla u).$$ We prove that at least for non-negative functions these are equivalent concepts.

</details>


### [121] [Normalized nonlinear Schrödinger ground states on defected grids with unbounded defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09908)
*William Borrelli,Nicolò Cangiotti,Simone Dovetta,Lorenzo Tentarelli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维方形网格上带无界缺陷的非线性薛定谔能量归一化基态，证明无界缺陷不一定破坏维度交叉，并揭示一维通道可主导变分问题导致紧性缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无界缺陷如何影响二维离散图上的维度交叉现象，以及一维组分对归一化基态存在性的影响，并考察该机制对图局部几何的敏感性。

Method: 结合小质量能量渐近分析与大质量爆破（blow-up）论证，在网格、半网格、四分之一网格及含二维区域和一维无界通道的缺陷图上进行变分分析。

Result: 半网格和四分之一网格上维度交叉持续存在，说明无界缺陷不必然破坏该现象；在二维区域与无界一维通道共存的缺陷图上，质量过小或过大时归一化基态均不存在，表明一维组分可主导变分问题并导致紧性缺失；适当附加缺陷可保持小质量不存在性，同时恢复大质量基态的存在性。

Conclusion: 无界缺陷与维度交叉的关系依赖于具体几何结构；一维通道通过主导变分问题引发紧性缺失，而通过调控局部缺陷几何可部分恢复基态存在性，揭示了图几何对变分问题的关键影响。

Abstract: We study normalized ground states for the nonlinear Schrödinger energy on two-dimensional square grids with unbounded defects. We first show that unbounded defects do not necessarily destroy the dimensional crossover, proving its persistence on the half-grid and the quarter-grid. We then consider a prototypical defected grid where a macroscopically two-dimensional region coexists with an unbounded one-dimensional channel. On this graph, normalized ground states do not exist for sufficiently small or sufficiently large masses, showing that the one-dimensional component can dominate the variational problem and induce loss of compactness. Finally, we show that this mechanism is sensitive to the local geometry of the graph: suitable additional defects preserve small-mass nonexistence but restore the existence of ground states for large masses. Our analysis combines small-mass energy asymptotics with a large-mass blow-up argument.

</details>


### [122] [Stability for a formally determined Lorentzian inverse problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10413)
*Spyridon Filippas,Lauri Oksanen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究洛伦兹流形上波方程(□_g+q)u=0中时空依赖势q的反问题，在零锥叶状结构假设下证明了形式确定型问题的Lipschitz稳定性，并由此得到该假设下洛伦兹Calderón问题中q的唯一性，方法是改造Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法并结合畸变平面波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹几何中的反问题（如Calderón问题）通常比椭圆情形更困难，稳定性与唯一性结果相对缺乏；本文旨在在较自然的零锥叶状结构假设下，建立时空依赖势的形式确定反问题的定量稳定性，并推广到唯一性结论。

Method: 采用改造的Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法（通常基于Carleman估计），并引入畸变平面波作为关键工具，在零锥叶状结构假设下处理形式确定版本的反问题。

Result: 在零锥叶状结构假设下，证明了形式确定反问题关于势q的Lipschitz稳定性估计；作为推论，得到该假设下洛伦兹Calderón问题中q的唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了洛伦兹波方程势反问题在零锥叶状结构条件下的稳定性和唯一性结果，展示了Bukhgeim–Klibanov方法与畸变平面波结合在处理洛伦兹反问题中的有效性。

Abstract: We consider the inverse problem of recovering a potential $q$, depending on space and time, in the wave equation $(\Box_g+q)u=0$ on a Lorentzian manifold. We prove Lipschitz stability for a formally determined version of this problem under a \textit{null-cone foliation} assumption. As a consequence, we obtain uniqueness in the Lorentzian Calderón problem for $q$ under this assumption. Our approach is based on a modification of the Bukhgeim--Klibanov method using distorted plane waves.

</details>


### [123] [Asymptotic stability of the 2D Boussinesq equations around Couette flow in infinite channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10005)
*Qionglei Chen,Jiakun Jin,Zhen Li,Changxing Miao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究Navier-slip边界条件下二维Boussinesq系统在无限通道中Couette流附近的稳定性阈值，证明了温度-涡度耦合下的小初值全局渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究温度-涡度耦合所导致的非线性echo链对Boussinesq系统在Couette流附近稳定性的影响，并确定其在Navier-slip边界条件下的稳定性阈值。

Method: 构造两级频率依赖的时间分解，分别对应方程的无穷叠加原理与有限叠加原理，并在各分解层级充分利用增强耗散效应来抑制非线性echo链。

Result: 若初值扰动满足 ‖ω^in‖_{H^4∩L^1_xH^4_y} ≤ cν^{1/3} 且 ‖θ^in‖_{H^5∩L^1_xH^5_y} ≤ cν^{2/3+}，则Boussinesq系统存在全局渐近稳定的解。

Conclusion: 该两级时间分解方法为处理具有耦合效应的流体力学方程提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stability threshold of the two-dimensional Boussinesq system around the Couette flow in an infinite channel $\mathbb{R}\times[-1,1]$ under Navier-slip boundary condition. To address nonlinear echo chains induced by temperature-vorticity coupling, we construct a two-level frequency-dependent temporal decomposition, with the two levels matched to the infinite and finite superposition principles for the equation, respectively. By fully exploiting the enhanced dissipation effect at each decomposition level, we prove that if the initial perturbation $(ω^{in}, θ^{in})$ around the Couette flow satisfies $\|ω^{in}\|_{H^4_{x,y}\cap L^1_xH^4_y}\leq cν^{\frac{1}{3}}$, and $\|θ^{in}\|_{H^5_{x,y}\cap L^1_xH^5_y}\leq cν^{\frac{2}{3}+}$, the Boussinesq system admits a globally asymptotically stable solution. It should be emphasized that the method in this paper provides an effective approach for the hydrodynamic equations with coupling effects.

</details>


### [124] [Well-posedness of Strong Solutions for a Thermodynamically Consistent Diffuse-interface Model for Incompressible Two-phase Flows with a Soluble Surfactant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10009)
*Maurizio Grasselli,Bohan Ouyang,Hao Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究含可溶性表面活性剂的两相不可压缩粘性牛顿流体混合物的扩散界面模型，建立了三维局部与二维整体强解的存在性、唯一性及相分离性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Abels-Garcke-Grün 模型推广到含可溶性表面活性剂、且迁移率依赖于浓度的情形，以刻画更一般的流体-表面活性剂耦合动力学，并推进强解理论。

Method: 在不可压缩 Navier-Stokes 方程与 Cahn-Hilliard 型相场方程耦合系统中加入表面活性剂浓度的 Cahn-Hilliard 方程；对流体速度施加无滑移边界条件，对相场变量与化学势施加齐次 Neumann 边界条件；在奇异势下分析初始边值问题的强适定性。

Result: 证明三维局部强解与二维整体强解的存在性；在二维中对奇异势给出自然增长假设下强解与纯相严格分离从而保证唯一性；在三维中证明初始数据与纯相严格分离时强解的局部唯一性。

Conclusion: 结果将 Abels-Garcke-Grün 模型强解理论从常数迁移率的特殊情形推广到浓度依赖迁移率并与表面活性剂相互作用的一般情形。

Abstract: We consider a thermodynamically consistent diffuse-interface model for binary mixtures of incompressible viscous Newtonian fluids with a soluble surfactant. This hydrodynamic system generalizes the Abels-Garcke-Grün model for two-phase flows with unmatched densities by incorporating an additional Cahn-Hilliard equation for the surfactant concentration. We study the strong well-posedness of the initial-boundary value problem with physically relevant singular potentials, subject to a no-slip boundary condition for the fluid velocity and homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions for the phase-field variables and the corresponding chemical potentials. First, we establish the existence of local-in-time strong solutions in three dimensions and global-in-time strong solutions in two dimensions. In the two-dimensional case, we further show that, under natural growth assumptions on the singular potentials, strong solutions remain strictly separated from the pure phases, thereby guaranteeing uniqueness. In the three-dimensional case, we establish local-in-time uniqueness of strong solutions, provided that the initial data are strictly separated from the pure phases. Our results extend previous work on strong solutions to the Abels-Garcke-Grün model from the special case of constant mobility to the more general scenario with concentration-dependent mobilities and interactions with a surfactant.

</details>


### [125] [A priori Dirichlet energy bounds for positive Trudinger--Moser critical points on simply connected planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10086)
*Hongtao Hu,Qiaoran Wu,Wenming Zou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在一类方程正解上Dirichlet能量的先验一致有界性，并由此否定了大能量下Trudinger--Moser泛函的非负约束临界点存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究指数非线性临界增长方程的先验估计与变分临界点问题，特别是控制能量无界情形下的解结构。

Method: 结合共形变换后的加权Pohozaev恒等式、归一化测度的集中分析以及局部径向比较；比较假设由局部积分估计导出，不依赖先验能量界；再用外部Green位势估计排除能量无界序列。

Result: 证明对任意θ>0，零Dirichlet边值下方程的正解其Dirichlet能量仅依赖于区域Ω而有界；因此Trudinger--Moser泛函在足够大的给定能量下不存在非负约束临界点。

Conclusion: 该结果表明此类临界指数方程的变分解在指定大能量下不会出现，Dirichlet能量具有区域依赖的先验上界。

Abstract: \noindent Let \(\Om\subset\R^2\) be a smooth bounded simply connected domain. We prove that all positive solutions of \(-Δu=θue^{u^2}\), with \(θ>0\) and zero Dirichlet boundary data, have Dirichlet energy bounded by a constant depending only on \(\Om\). Consequently, the Trudinger--Moser functional has no nonnegative constrained critical points at sufficiently large prescribed energies. The proof combines a weighted Pohozaev identity obtained after conformal change of variables, concentration analysis of normalized measures, and local radial comparison. The comparison hypotheses are obtained from local integral estimates, without an a priori energy bound. An estimate for the exterior Green potential then excludes sequences of unbounded energy.

</details>


### [126] [A proof of the fractional Lane--Emden conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10188)
*Linfeng Mei,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了分数阶 Lane–Emden 系统 $(-\Delta)^s u=v^p,\ (-\Delta)^s v=u^q$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上的 Liouville 定理：当 $\frac{1}{p+1}+\frac{1}{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}$ 时不存在严格正的整体解，且不假设径向对称、全局有界、有限能量或衰减性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶 Lane–Emden 系统的 Liouville 型不存在性结果通常依赖径向对称、全局有界、有限能量或无穷远衰减等额外假设。作者希望去除这些限制，将最近为经典 Hénon–Lane–Emden 系统发展的局部化势核 virial 与源层区间压力方法推广到分数阶情形，从而在自然次临界条件下得到一般性不存在性定理。

Method: 方法核心是局部化势核 virial 与源层区间压力方法。关键观察是 $(-\Delta)^s$ 对应的 Riesz 核与 Newton 核具有相同的对数导数结构，只需将 $n-2$ 替换为 $n-2s$。两个非局部困难需特殊处理：当 $2s\le1$ 时单个核梯度项非局部绝对可积，故须先合并两式再取对角极限；当 $2s>n-1$ 时核在直线上无穷远不可积，区间比较须用非负扩展积分表述。解决后，通过局部化 virial 恒等式、一维区间对不等式与平均源层估计得到统一的局部能量界，次临界伸缩使该能量发散，导出矛盾。

Result: 证明了 Liouville 定理：若 $\frac{1}{p+1}+\frac{1}{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}$，则系统在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 中不存在严格正的整体解；该结论无需径向对称、全局有界、有限能量或无穷远衰减假设。技术上建立了局部化 virial 恒等式、一维区间对不等式和平均源层估计，并得到统一的局部能量界，与次临界伸缩下的能量发散相矛盾。

Conclusion: 作者成功将势核 virial 与源层区间压力方法推广到分数阶 Lane–Emden 系统，克服了低阶非局部绝对可积性与高阶核无穷远不可积性两个本质困难，在自然次临界指数条件下建立了无需额外对称或衰减假设的 Liouville 定理。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$, $0<s<1$, and $p,q>0$. We prove a Liouville theorem for the fractional Lane--Emden system \[
  (-Δ)^s u=v^p,\qquad (-Δ)^s v=u^q, \qquad u, v>0
  \qquad\text{in }\R^n. \] More precisely, we show that the system does not have a strictly positive entire solution whenever \[
  \frac1{p+1}+\frac1{q+1}>\frac{n-2s}{n}. \] No radial symmetry, global boundedness, finite-energy assumption, or prescribed decay at infinity is required. Our proof adapts the localized potential-kernel virial and source-layer interval-pressure method recently introduced for the classical Hénon--Lane--Emden system. The basic reason the method also works in the fractional setting is simple: the Riesz kernel associated with $(-Δ)^s$ has the same logarithmic derivative structure as the Newton kernel, with $n-2$ replaced by $n-2s$. However, two genuinely nonlocal points require special treatments. When $2s\le1$, the individual kernel-gradient terms are not locally absolutely integrable, so the two equations must be combined before the diagonal limit is taken. When $2s>n-1$, the kernel restricted to a line is not integrable at infinity, and the interval comparison must therefore be formulated using nonnegative extended integrals. After these issues are resolved, a localized virial identity, a one-dimensional interval-pair inequality, and an averaged source-layer estimate yield a universal local energy bound. The subcritical scaling then forces the same energy to diverge, giving the desired contradiction.

</details>


### [127] [The stability of Sharp Poincaré and Brascamp--Lieb inequalities with full monomial weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10337)
*Nguyen Lam,Guozhen Lu,Andrey Russanov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究全单项式权重高斯测度的曲率-维数条件与泛函不等式，建立ICD(2,∞)并证明最优常数2的Poincaré不等式及Brascamp–Lieb稳定性，进而得到改进Beckner不等式与精确Poincaré亏损恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知单项式权重高斯测度满足逐点CD(1,∞)条件，但只能对部分单项式权重导出Poincaré不等式的最优常数1，无法处理全单项式权重情形，因此需要引入更弱的积分曲率-维数条件来获得最优常数与稳定性结果。

Method: 建立全单项式权重高斯测度的积分曲率-维数条件ICD(2,∞)；结合超压缩性推导改进Beckner不等式；发展相关Ornstein–Uhlenbeck型生成元的Laguerre谱分解；在尺度不变Hessian条件下改进积分曲率-维数界。

Result: 证明全单项式高斯测度满足ICD(2,∞)并给出最优常数2的sharp Poincaré不等式与Brascamp–Lieb不等式稳定性；部分结果推广至凸锥上齐次高斯测度；得到可在端点恢复sharp Poincaré与对数Sobolev常数的改进Beckner不等式；建立Poincaré亏损的精确恒等式与梯度稳定性；当所有单项式指数大于1时给出Brascamp–Lieb的L²与加权梯度稳定性显式常数，L²估计可推广至次调和条件下的对数凹齐次权重；最后在径向类中确定Poincaré不等式的最优常数。

Conclusion: 该工作通过积分曲率-维数框架系统刻画了全单项式（及齐次）高斯测度的泛函不等式最优常数与稳定性，谱分解方法为Poincaré亏损提供了精确结构，并为Brascamp–Lieb与Beckner型不等式建立了显式稳定性估计。

Abstract: It was known that the Gaussian measures with monomial weights satisfy the sharp pointwise curvature-dimension condition $CD(1, \infty)$, and consequently they allow us to derive the sharp constant $1$ for the Poincaré inequality only with partial monomial weights. In this paper, we first establish the Gaussian measures with full monomial weights satisfy the integrated curvature-dimension condition $ICD(2, \infty)$ which is sufficient for us to prove the sharp Poincaré inequalities with optimal constant $2$ and the stability of the Brascamp--Lieb inequality for full monomial Gaussian measures. Several of these results are extended to homogeneous Gaussian measures on convex cones. Using the sharp Poincaré inequality and the hypercontractivity, we obtain an improved Beckner inequality that recover the sharp Poincaré and logarithmic Sobolev constants at the endpoints. By developing a Laguerre spectral decomposition of the associated Ornstein--Uhlenbeck type generator, we derive an exact identity for the Poincaré deficit and obtain sharp gradient stability for the Poincaré inequality with full monomial weights. We further establish $L^2$ and weighted gradient stability estimates for the Brascamp--Lieb inequality with explicit constants when all monomial exponents exceed $1$. The $L^2$ estimate extends to log-concave homogeneous weights under a subharmonicity assumption. Finally, we obtain an improved integrated curvature-dimension bound under a scale-invariant Hessian condition and identify the sharp constant for the Poincaré inequality in the radial class.

</details>


### [128] [Uniqueness and stability of planar shock wave to 2D compressible Euler equations in the vanishing viscosity limit of compressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10341)
*Geng Chen,Jeffrey Cheng,Alexis F. Vasseur,Yi Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文引入二维可压缩Navier-Stokes方程（密度依赖粘性）全局弱解框架，建立了首个针对二维可压缩Euler方程平面激波解的消失粘性极限选择准则，证明了该激波解在近平面初值（激波方向扰动可任意大）的粘性解极限类中的唯一性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可压缩Euler方程在平面激波初值下可能有无穷多熵弱解（Chiodaroli–De Lellis–Kreml, 2015），因此需要寻找合适的解选择准则来筛选出物理相关的解。消失粘性极限是自然的选择机制，但此前缺乏针对二维的严格证明。

Method: 在R×T上引入合适的全局弱解概念。以Bresch–Noble–Vila在T²上构造的相对κ-熵为基础，建立二维Navier-Stokes弱解与一维平均初值强解之间的相对1/2-熵。通过弥合原始Navier-Stokes系统二维Euler表述下的相对1/2-熵与二维消失粘性极限之间的结构性差距，将其联系到Kang–Vasseur在Lagrange坐标下Euler方程激波解的一维消失粘性极限结果。

Result: 证明了二维Euler平面激波解在一类由近平面初值出发的Navier-Stokes解的不粘极限中的唯一性与稳定性，且激波方向扰动可以任意大。

Conclusion: 建立了物理相关平面激波解的首个消失粘性极限选择准则。该框架可推广至R×T²的三维极限选择准则，以及更复杂的平面Riemann解和相互作用波结构。

Abstract: It is well known that the compressible Euler equations with planar shock initial data may admit infinitely many entropy weak solutions, as shown by Chiodaroli, De Lellis and Kreml [Comm. Pure Appl. Math. (2015)]. In this paper, we introduce a suitable notion of global-in-time weak solutions to the two-dimensional (2D) compressible Navier-Stokes equations with density-dependent viscosities in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}$. Within this solution framework, we establish the first vanishing viscosity limit selection criterion for the physically relevant planar shock solution to the 2D compressible Euler equations. More precisely, we prove the uniqueness and stability of the 2D Euler planar shock solution within a class of inviscid limits of Navier-Stokes solutions emanating from near-planar initial data while the shock direction perturbation can be arbitrarily large. Starting from the relative $κ$-entropy for 2D compressible Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb{T}^2$ by Bresch, Noble and Vila [ESAIM Proc. Surveys (2017)], we first establish the relative $\frac12$-entropy between 2D weak solution of compressible Navier-Stokes equations in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}$ and one-dimensional (1D) strong solution for averaged initial data in $\mathbb{R}$. Then we achieve the desired vanishing viscosity limit selection criterion by successfully bridging the fundamental structural gap between 2D Eulerian formulation of the original Navier-Stokes system in the relative $\frac12$-entropy and 1D vanishing viscosity limit to a shock solution of Euler equations in Lagrangian coordinates by Kang and Vasseur [Invent. Math. (2021)]. The framework developed herein can be applied to the three-dimensional limit selection criterion in $\mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{T}^2$, as well as to more complex planar Riemann solutions and interacting wave patterns.

</details>


### [129] [Asymptotic Stability of Domain Walls for One Dimensional Schrödinger Map with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10458)
*Ze Li,Zifan Wang,Lifeng Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究含恒定Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的一维Schrödinger映射中静态畴壁小扰动在加权Sobolev空间中的长时间动力学，证明调制参数收敛、辐射按t^{-1/2}衰减，并揭示带对数相位修正的修正散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一维Schrödinger映射中畴壁这类拓扑结构的长期稳定性及辐射行为，尤其是扰动方程含导数非线性且线性化算子存在阈值共振时，传统方法难以直接处理。

Method: 在加权Sobolev空间中分析扰动方程；针对导数非线性带来的导数损失，采用几何约化方法，并利用非线性相互作用中的结构对消；处理线性化算子的阈值共振。

Result: 证明畴壁在平移和旋转对称意义下渐近稳定：调制参数随时间趋于无穷时收敛，辐射以尖锐速率t^{-1/2}衰减；进一步证明辐射呈现修正散射，由长程三次相互作用产生显式对数相位修正。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用的一维Schrödinger映射畴壁的渐近稳定性与修正散射理论，几何约化与结构对消为处理导数非线性和阈值共振提供有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the long time dynamics of small perturbations of static domain walls in weighted Sobolev spaces for the one dimensional Schrödinger map with constant Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction. The perturbation equation contains derivative nonlinearities, and its linearized operator has a threshold resonance. The results are twofold. (i) We prove the asymptotic stability of the domain wall modulo the translation and rotation symmetries. More precisely, the corresponding modulation parameters converge as time tends to infinity, while the radiation decays at the sharp rate $t^{-1/2}$. (ii) We prove that the radiation exhibits modified scattering, with an explicit logarithmic phase correction generated by the long range cubic interaction. The proof develops geometric reduction to overcome the loss of derivatives and exploits structural cancellations in the nonlinear interactions.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [130] [Mean Estimates for Short Polynomial Exponential Sums over Primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08886)
*Karimjon Ibrohimjonovich Mirzoabdughafurov*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究素数上指数和均值 $V_n(K,x,y)$，对 $\alpha=a/q+\theta/q^2$ 建立统一上界，并给出对数幂次节省的充分条件及整数情形的类似均值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需在短区间素数上估计 Weyl 型指数和的多重均值，这类量在圆法、素数分布与短区间问题中有用；目标是对任意 $\alpha$ 的 Diophantine 近似获得统一、精细的界，并揭示对数幂次节省条件。

Method: 采用非负 Fejér 核、逐次差分技术，结合广义除数函数的二阶与三阶矩来估计相关均值；用 Brun–Titchmarsh 不等式控制区间内素数个数，从而在平均项中获得额外对数节省；最终得到对区间位置一致、对数因子仅依赖区间长度的界。

Result: 建立 $V_n(K,x,y)\ll Ky[1/\sqrt{K\log(2y)}+\min\{\Delta^{1/2^n}(\log(2y))^{(n^2-1)/2^n},\Delta^{1/(3\cdot 2^{n-2})}(\log(2y))^{(n^3-1)/(3\cdot 2^{n-1})}\}]$，其中 $\Delta=1/q+1/y+q/(Ky^n)$；给出对区间长度对数任意固定幂次节省的充分条件，并得到整数上求和的类似均值估计。

Conclusion: 所得界一致适用于区间位置，对数因子依赖区间长度；方法可统一处理素数情形和整数情形的均值，并提供对数幂次节省的判据。

Abstract: Let $n\ge2$ be a fixed integer, and let $K,x,y$ be positive integers satisfying $2\le K\le y<x$. Consider a polynomial \[ f(u)=αu^n+α_{n-1}u^{n-1}+\cdots+α_1u+α_0 \] with real coefficients. We study the quantity \[ V_n(K,x,y) = \sum_{k=1}^{K} \left| \sum_{x-y<p\le x}e(kf(p)) \right|, \qquad e(t)=e^{2πit}, \] where the inner sum is over primes. For $α=\frac{a}{q}+\fracθ{q^2}$, $a\in\mathbb Z$, $q\in\mathbb N$, $(a,q)=1$, $|θ|\le1$, we establish the bound \[ \begin{aligned} V_n(K,x,y) &\ll Ky\Biggl[ \frac{1}{\sqrt{K\log(2y)}}+ \min\left\{ Δ^{\frac{1}{2^n}} \bigl(\log(2y)\bigr)^{\frac{n^2-1}{2^n}}, Δ^{\frac{1}{3\cdot 2^{n-2}}} \bigl(\log(2y)\bigr)^{\frac{n^3-1}{3\cdot 2^{n-1}}} \right\} \Biggr], \end{aligned} \] where \[ Δ= \frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{y}+\frac{q}{Ky^n}. \] The bound is uniform in the position of the interval, and the logarithmic factors depend on its length. The proof uses the nonnegative Fejér kernel, successive differencing, and the second and third moments of the generalized divisor function. The Brun-Titchmarsh inequality accounts for the number of primes in the interval and provides an additional logarithmic saving in the term arising from averaging. We obtain sufficient conditions for a saving of any fixed power of the logarithm of the interval length, as well as an analogous mean estimate for sums over all integers.

</details>


### [131] [Consecutive Cycle Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08889)
*Jessica Marotti,Jonathan Needleman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究环形排列连续求和问题，证明当 n≥8 时，n-循环均不完备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将 1 到 n 依次放在 n-循环上时，哪些整数 k 可表示为环上若干连续数之和；并考察能否覆盖 1 到 T_n 的所有整数（完备性）这一自然组合数论问题。

Method: 将 1,…,n 依次置于 n-循环；定义“连续和”为环上相邻连续段的元素和。若所有 1 至 T_n 的整数都能表示为该连续和，则称 n-循环完备。通过组合构造与不可表示性分析研究完备性的充要条件或性质。

Result: 主要结果为：对任意 n≥8，n-循环不完备，即存在 1 与 T_n 之间的某个整数无法写成环上连续数之和。

Conclusion: n-循环的完备性仅在 n≤7 时可能成立；当 n≥8 时完备性失效。

Abstract: Consider placing the numbers $1, \ldots, n$ consecutively around an $n$-cycle. We ask for a given $n$, which numbers $k$ can be written as a sum of consecutive numbers on the $n$-cycle. If all numbers between $1$ and $T_n$, the $n$th triangular number, can be written as a sum this way we say the $n$-cycle is complete. Our main result shows that for $n\geq 8$, $n$-cycles are incomplete.

</details>


### [132] [More than 83.9% of the zeros of the Riemann zeta function are distinct and more than 67.35% are simple and on the critical line](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08965)
*Kristian Muri Knausgård*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点中相异零点与临界线上单零点的下界比例，并证明关于此类不等式能力上限的结果，全部在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼猜想背景下，Montgomery配对相关定理给出零点对能量的渐近信息。多重零点对能量贡献为d²，故若能压低多重零点所占能量份额，即可推出相异零点/单零点比例的下界。此前工作（包括Lamzouri的估计）已有若干比例结果，本文旨在进一步改进这些常数并把证明形式化，同时检验AI辅助数学研究的可行性。

Method: 利用Montgomery配对相关定理的无条件形式：测试函数在零点对上的和（能量）渐近已知。证明分三步：(1)对间距至少为平均间距固定比例的零点，用大筛不等式使其配对可带重数完整使用；(2)用关于七或八个连续零点的计算机辅助不等式（区分单零点与双零点）给出该贡献的下界，其修正项作为存储函数增量可望远镜求和；(3)选取测试函数使该贡献尽量大（代价是能量增大）。此外还证明在两种主要测试函数下此类不等式可达到的上限。全部下界与上限在Lean 4中形式化验证，使用Mathlib之外的三条公理（各对应一个不等式，记录搜索程序返回true，Lean内核不检查程序运行本身）。

Result: liminf N_d(T)/N(T) ≥ 1645064/1960733 = 0.83900…（此前0.83699…，未经核实报告称0.83805…），即超过67.80%的零点为单零点；liminf N_0^s(T)/N(T) ≥ 0.67353…（此前0.67250…，未经核实报告称0.673492）。改进Lamzouri两个估计：至少88.93%的零点为单零点或位于临界线上；单零点比例与线上零点比例的均值至少为83.67%。

Conclusion: 论文给出相异零点比例与临界线上单零点比例的新下界（0.83900…与0.67353…），改进了此前结果及Lamzouri的估计，并证明了两种主要测试函数下该类方法的上限。所有结论连同解析输入均在Lean 4中形式化，仅依赖三条记录搜索程序返回true的公理。作为一项AI辅助数学研究实验，其意义在于展示形式化验证在高难解析数论结果中的应用与边界。

Abstract: Let N(T) count the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function up to height T with multiplicity, N_d(T) the distinct ones, and N_0^s(T) those that are simple and on the critical line. We prove liminf N_d(T)/N(T) >= 1645064/1960733 = 0.83900...; earlier work proves 0.83699..., and a report we have not verified claims 0.83805.... Hence more than 67.80% are simple. Also liminf N_0^s(T)/N(T) >= 0.67353...; earlier work proves 0.67250..., and reports we have not verified claim 0.673492. Two estimates of Lamzouri are improved: at least 88.93% of the zeros are simple or on the critical line, and the proportions of simple zeros and of zeros on the line average at least 83.67%. By the unconditional form of Montgomery's pair-correlation theorem the energy, a sum of a test function over pairs of zeros, is known asymptotically. A zero of multiplicity d contributes d^2 to it and nearby zeros contribute too, so a lower bound for such pairs leaves less energy for multiple zeros. The proof has three steps. First, for zeros at least a fixed fraction of the mean spacing apart, a large sieve inequality lets their pairs be used in full, with multiplicities. Second, their contribution is bounded below by a computer-assisted inequality for seven or eight consecutive zeros which distinguishes simple and double zeros. Its correction terms telescope, as increments of a storage function. Third, the test function is chosen to make this contribution large, at the cost of more energy. We also prove upper limits for what such inequalities can give with the two main test functions. The lower bounds and these limits are proved in Lean 4, analytic inputs included. The proofs use three axioms beyond those of Mathlib, one per inequality. Each records that a search program returned true; Lean's kernel does not check the run itself. This work is an experiment in AI-assisted mathematical research.

</details>


### [133] [Automorphisms of general modular curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08998)
*Valerio Dose,Guido Maria Lido,Pietro Mercuri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了素数水平大于311的几何连通模曲线X_H的所有自同构均为模自同构，并给出任意水平下的计算算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定模曲线自同构是否都来自其模解释（即模自同构），是模曲线算术几何中的基本问题，需要新的判据与计算方法。

Method: 利用涉及X_H的gonality（gonal数/射影度）与X_H的Jacobian的CM部分维数的通用判据；对任意水平给出计算模自同构并在许多情形证明不存在其他自同构的算法。

Result: 对素数水平大于311的几何连通模曲线X_H，证明所有自同构都是模自同构；对任意水平得到计算模自同构的算法，并在许多情况下证明无其他自同构。

Conclusion: 模曲线的自同构在足够大素数水平下完全由模解释给出，且所提算法可应用于LMFDB数据库中的曲线。

Abstract: We prove that for a geometrically connected modular curve $X_H$ of prime level larger than $311$, all automorphisms are modular, i.e., they arise from the moduli interpretation of $X_H$. The proof relies on a general criterion involving the gonality of $X_H$ and the dimension of the CM part of the Jacobian of $X_H$. For arbitrary level, we give algorithms to compute the modular automorphisms and, in many cases, to prove that there are no other automorphisms. Finally, we apply these algorithms to the curves in the database LMFDB.

</details>


### [134] [Affine Coordinate Subspaces and Strong Khintchine Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09017)
*Zirui Jiang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了任意正维数、正余维数的仿射坐标子空间均不是（收敛意义下）强Khintchine型，并结合Huang的判别准则给出一个充分丢番图条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究仿射子空间在丢番图逼近中的Khintchine型性质，特别是区分"Khintchine型"与"强Khintchine型（收敛意义）"两类性质，厘清仿射坐标子空间的分类。

Method: 利用Huang关于Khintchine型仿射子空间的判别准则，结合对仿射坐标子空间的具体构造与分析（反例/证明技术）。

Result: 证明了任意正维数且正余维数的仿射坐标子空间都不是收敛意义下的强Khintchine型；由此得到一个仿射坐标子空间属于Khintchine型但不属于强Khintchine型的充分丢番图条件。

Conclusion: 仿射坐标子空间在强Khintchine型性质上存在普遍性失败，Huang判别准则可用于刻画Khintchine型与强Khintchine型之间的差异。

Abstract: We prove that every affine coordinate subspace of positive dimension and positive codimension fails to be of strong Khintchine type for convergence. Combining this with Huang's criterion for Khintchine-type affine subspaces gives a sufficient Diophantine condition for an affine coordinate subspace to be of Khintchine type but not of strong Khintchine type for convergence.

</details>


### [135] [Idempotence in Carmichael and Giuga numbers and its application to the Agoh-Giuga conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09129)
*Miguel Ángel López*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究Giuga数与Carmichael数的幂等性，定义新的算术函数以给出Giuga数的新刻画，推广Carmichael数并探讨Fermat伪素数情形，最后提出基于幂等性的Agoh-Giuga猜想反例的新等价条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Giuga数与Carmichael数均为无平方因子数，具有共同的算术结构。作者希望通过幂等性（idempotence）这一代数视角统一分析二者，寻找新的刻画方式，并围绕Agoh-Giuga猜想提出新的判别条件。

Method: 引入一个算术函数作为核心工具，利用幂等性对Giuga数进行重新刻画；在此框架下推广Carmichael数，构造在特定情形下为Fermat伪素数的数；并推导Agoh-Giuga猜想反例所需满足的基于幂等性的新替代条件。多数结果在n为无平方因子的前提下讨论，部分结论推广至一般情形。

Result: 得到Giuga数的一个新刻画；获得Carmichael数的推广，找到特定情形下的Fermat伪素数并提出若干命题与开放问题；给出Agoh-Giuga猜想反例必须满足的基于幂等性的新替代条件。

Conclusion: 幂等性为联系Giuga数与Carmichael数提供了有效工具，既给出已知对象的重新刻画，也引出新的推广与开放问题；所提出的幂等性条件表明Agoh-Giuga猜想的反例预计不存在，暗示该猜想成立。

Abstract: This article analyzes the idempotence of Giuga numbers and Carmichael numbers. The first sections will lead us to define an arithmetic function through which we will find a new characterization of Giuga numbers. The following sections will lead us to generalize Carmichael numbers, finding numbers that are Fermat pseudoprimes in certain specific cases, and to formulate various statements and open questions regarding their properties. Finally, we will find a new alternative condition, based on idempotence, that a number must satisfy to be a counterexample to the Agoh-Giuga conjecture and which, therefore, presumably, no number will ever satisfy. We will almost always work under the premise that n is square-free, since both numbers always have that property, but in certain situations we will extend some of the results to the general case.

</details>


### [136] [Galois descents of certain multiple polylogarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16735)
*Yajun Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Au关于分圆层级下降的若干猜想，并在若干特殊情形下回答了Au的问题，进而证实了Broadhurst关于偶权重荣誉多重zeta值的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Au曾提出关于多重对数函数之和中分圆层级下降的猜想，并就此提出若干问题；同时Broadhurst对偶权重下的荣誉多重zeta值提出猜想，这些均有待严格证明。

Method: 通过研究多重对数函数之和中分圆层级的下降性质，在若干特殊情形下给出Au所提问题的解答，并据此处理偶权重情形。

Result: 证明了K. C. Au关于分圆层级下降的若干猜想，并证实了Broadhurst关于偶权重荣誉多重zeta值的猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了Au的猜想与Broadhurst猜想在偶权重情形下的问题，推动了多重对数函数与多重zeta值相关理论的发展。

Abstract: We prove some conjectures of K. C. Au concerning the descents of cyclotomic levels in certain sums over multiple polylogarithms. Via answers to a question of Au in some particular cases, we also confirm Broadhurst's conjecture on honorary multiple zeta values at even weights.

</details>


### [137] [On a Digital Signature Based on Matrix Decomposition and the MinRank Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09140)
*Miguel Ángel López*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MinRank问题变体的新型数字签名方案，给出了密钥生成、签名与验证流程，分析了签名/验证速度，并从抗已知攻击的角度进行安全性评估，据此确定参数选取要求；该方案设计简单，可作为同类签名族的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 借助MinRank问题变体构造数字签名，以探索其在效率与安全性之间的平衡，并期望以其核心思想衍生出一整类数字签名方案。

Method: 在适当参数下定义基于MinRank问题变体的签名方案：首先定义密钥生成过程，随后定义消息签名与验证过程，并分析签名和验证的速度。

Result: 给出了签名与验证的效率分析，并从对已知攻击的安全性/鲁棒性角度进行评估，进而得到关于变量规模与数量等相关参数选取的一组要求。

Conclusion: 该签名方案虽然设计简单，但可作为基于其核心思想的一整类数字签名方案的基础。

Abstract: The following article attempts to define a new digital signature scheme based on a variant of the MinRank problem under appropriate parameters. First, the key generation process will be defined, followed by the message signing and verification processes. The speed of signing and verification will be analyzed, and finally, the signature will be evaluated in terms of security and robustness against known attacks, which will lead to a set of requirements that we will consider when selecting certain parameters related to the size and number of variables. It should be noted that this signature, although simple in design, can serve as the foundation for an entire family of digital signatures based on the central idea that inspired its creation.

</details>


### [138] [On the algebraic and analytic ranks of the twin-prime elliptic curve $y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09370)
*Ruihan Chen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究孪生素数 $p,p-2$ 对应的椭圆曲线 $E_p:y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$ 的 $2$-Selmer 群与 BSD 相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用 Qiu-Zhang 的经典 $2$-Selmer 计算与 Cassels-Tate 配对，刻画 $E_p$ 的 $2^\infty$-Selmer 余秩，并在低余秩 BSD 假设下推导算术不变量。

Method: 结合 $2$-Selmer 计算与 Cassels-Tate 配对，附录给出局部下降证明已知 Selmer 维数。

Result: $p\equiv 3,5\pmod 8$ 时 $2^\infty$-Selmer 余秩为一，$p\equiv 7\pmod 8$ 时余秩为零；在低余秩 BSD 假设下得到解析秩与代数秩相等、Tate-Shafarevich 群有限及 BSD 首项公式；$2$-primary Tate-Shafarevich 群平凡，其余因子为奇平方阶。

Conclusion: 精确识别了 $p\equiv 1\pmod 8$ 情形所剩余的障碍，并给出相应 Selmer 维数的局部下降证明。

Abstract: Let $p\ge 7$ and suppose that $p$ and $p-2$ are prime. We study $E_p:y^2=x(x-2)(x-p)$ using the classical $2$-Selmer calculation of Qiu-Zhang and the Cassels-Tate pairing. These give $2^\infty$-Selmer corank one for $p\equiv 3,5\pmod{8}$ and corank zero for $p\equiv 7\pmod{8}$. Assuming the low-corank Birch-Swinnerton-Dyer statement announced in the October 2026 OpenAI mathematics release, we deduce equality of the analytic and algebraic ranks in these cases, finiteness of the full Tate-Shafarevich group, and the exact BSD leading-term formula. The $2$-primary Tate-Shafarevich group is trivial, and the remaining factor has odd square order. We identify precisely the obstruction left in the class $p\equiv 1\pmod{8}$. An appendix gives a local descent proof of the known Selmer dimensions in the coordinates used here.

</details>


### [139] [Regular integral models of Shimura varieties and a conjecture of Pappas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09475)
*Jie Yang,Ioannis Zachos,Zhihao Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了奇数素数p处极大仿射型parahoric层级的辛型和正交型Shimura簇的正规整模型，其特殊纤维具有正规交叉且不可约分支重数为1或2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为辛型和偶正交相似群在极大parahoric层级构造正规整模型，并证明Pappas提出的等变修改猜想。这些模型在算术几何与Langlands纲领中具有基本重要性，可用于研究Shimura簇的整性质与特殊纤维结构。

Method: 利用对应典则局部模型的显式等变修改，针对与极大迷向Grassmannian对应的minuscule余特征；这些修改通过对特殊纤维中的Schubert簇逐次爆破得到，覆盖辛型和分裂偶正交相似群两类情形。

Result: 构造的整模型定义在反射域的整数环上，特殊纤维具有正规交叉且不可约分支重数为1或2；由此在上述情形下证明了Pappas的等变修改猜想在极大parahoric层级成立。

Conclusion: 作者在极大parahoric层级为辛型和正交型Shimura簇建立了具良好特殊纤维性质的正规整模型，并验证了Pappas等变修改猜想，为后续研究更一般的parahoric层级及相应局部模型提供了方法与范例。

Abstract: We construct regular integral models for a class of Shimura varieties of symplectic and orthogonal type with maximal parahoric level at an odd prime $p$. These models are defined over the ring of integers of the reflex field and have special fiber with normal crossings and irreducible components of multiplicity one or two. Our construction uses explicit equivariant modifications of the corresponding canonical local models for symplectic and split even orthogonal similitude groups, with the minuscule cocharacters corresponding to maximal isotropic Grassmannians. These modifications are obtained by successively blowing up Schubert varieties in the special fiber. This proves the equivariant modification conjecture of Pappas at maximal parahoric level in the above cases.

</details>


### [140] [Higher order uniformity of the primes and cancellation of the Möbius function in shorter intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09567)
*Kaisa Matomäki,Mayank Pandey,Javier Pliego,Joni Teräväinen,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文改进了素数分布中 von Mangoldt 函数与 Möbius 函数在短区间内的均匀性与抵消性估计，核心是将三线性和的估计归约为 Dirichlet 多项式乘积均值的“分段线性函数正性”判定，并结合最新的 Guth–Maynard 大值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前 von Mangoldt 函数减去 Cramér 模型的 Gowers 均匀性仅在指数 5/8 的短区间内成立，Möbius 函数的抵消性仅在指数 11/20 内成立。作者希望借助近期大值估计的进展（Guth–Maynard）来缩小短区间长度，从而改进素数上线性方程等结论的适用范围。

Method: 方法上聚焦于短区间上的三线性和，将其归约为三个 Dirichlet 多项式乘积的均值估计；在一般框架下，把具有给定大值界的均值估计任务转化为验证某个分段线性函数为正，从而可纳入近期大值估计。结合 Guth–Maynard 的最新大值估计，证明了两类关键估计：长度 X^{19/35+ε} 区间上 type I/II 和的 Heath-Brown–Iwaniec 型估计，以及长度 X^{3/5+ε} 区间上一般三线性和的 Baker–Harman–Pintz 平行四边形型估计。

Result: 证明 von Mangoldt 函数减去 Cramér 模型在所有短区间 [X, X+X^{3/5+ε}] 上具有 Gowers 均匀性，从 5/8 改进到 3/5；由此得到此类短区间内素数线性方程的局部–整体定理。同时证明 Möbius 函数在所有区间 [X, X+X^{19/35+ε}] 上具有抵消性，从 11/20 改进到 19/35。

Conclusion: 通过将三线性和的均值估计归约为分段线性函数正性问题，并融入 Guth–Maynard 的大值估计，论文在短区间素数分布的两个经典指数上取得改进，为素数上线性方程等应用提供了更短的区间条件，并给出了可继续吸收未来大值估计进展的一般框架。

Abstract: We prove the Gowers uniformity of the von Mangoldt function minus its Cramér model in all short intervals $[X,X+X^{3/5+\varepsilon}]$, improving on the work of the first and fourth authors with Shao and Tao, where the exponent was $5/8$. This also implies a local-to-global theorem for linear equations in primes in such short intervals.
  We also show that the Möbius function has cancellation in all intervals $[X,X+X^{19/35+\varepsilon}]$, improving on the work of the first and fourth authors, where the exponent was $11/20$.
  Both improvements are based on improved treatment of trilinear sums over short intervals which naturally reduces to estimating mean values of products of three Dirichlet polynomials. In a general setting, we reduce the task of estimating mean values of products of Dirichlet polynomials with given large value bounds to the task of showing that a certain piecewise linear function is positive. This reduction allows recent large value estimates to be incorporated.
  Combining this general framework with the most recent large value estimates stemming from the work of Guth and Maynard, we prove a Heath-Brown--Iwaniec type estimate for type I/II sums in intervals of length $X^{19/35+\varepsilon}$ and a Baker--Harman--Pintz parallelogram type estimate for general trilinear sums in intervals of length $X^{3/5+\varepsilon}$.

</details>


### [141] [Prime points on smooth hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09735)
*Yijie Diao,Shuntaro Yamagishi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对次数 $d\geq 4$ 的整系数齐次型 $F$（定义 $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}_{\mathbb{C}}$ 中光滑超曲面），在 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$ 与适当局部条件下，证明了方程 $F(x_1,\dots,x_n)=0$ 的素数解数目的渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高次超曲面上素数点的分布问题（将二次型的素数渐近公式推广到 $d\geq 4$ 的光滑超曲面情形），需要克服高次方程带来的解析数论困难。

Method: 采用圆法（circle method）处理 $d\geq 4$ 的齐次方程，配合对光滑超曲面局部性质的利用与变量数充分大的条件（$n \gtrsim d^4 2^d$）以控制误差项。

Result: 在 $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$ 且满足适当局部条件时，建立了 $F(x_1,\dots,x_n)=0$ 素数解数目的渐近公式。

Conclusion: 高次光滑超曲面在变量数足够多时具有与奇异级数预测相符的素数解渐近分布，将相关素数渐近公式推广至 $d\geq 4$ 的一般齐次型。

Abstract: Let $F \in \mathbb{Z}[x_1, \ldots, x_n]$ be a homogeneous form of degree $d \geq 4$ which defines a smooth hypersurface in $\mathbb{P}^{n-1}_{\mathbb{C}}$. For $n \geq 24 d^4 2^d$, we prove an asymptotic formula for the number of prime solutions to the equation $F(x_1, \ldots, x_n) = 0$, provided $F$ satisfies suitable local conditions.

</details>


### [142] [Linear independence of values of polylogarithms with periodic coefficients, outside the disk of convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09774)
*Ludovic Mistiaen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对周期系数 Lerch 型级数 L(f,i,z₀)=Σ f(m)z₀^m/m^i 在代数点 z₀ 处的取值给出了 Q(z₀)-线性空间维数的下界，阶为 √(s/log s)，推广了 Fischler 关于多重对数（polylogarithm）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fischler 2026 年证明了 f≡1、|z₀|≤1 时多重对数取值 Li_i(z₀) 的线性无关性下界（阶 √(s/log s)）。本文希望将这一结果推广到更一般的周期函数 f 以及更广的 z₀ 范围（包括 |z₀|>1 经解析延拓的情形），从而刻画更广泛 Lerch 型级数取值的线性无关性。

Method: 1) 对周期 N 的函数 f 与不落在割线 [e^{2iℓπ/N}, e^{2iℓπ/N}∞[ 上的代数数 z₀，构造 L(f,i,z₀) 的线性组合；2) 使用 Siegel 引理的精细化版本来控制线性组合的系数；3) 应用推广的线性无关判别准则（Fischler 所用准则的推广）；4) 通过多重对数函数的积分表示与 Shidlovskii 型引理验证判别准则的假设。

Result: 证明了由 L(f,i,z₀)（i=1,…,s）所张成的 Q(z₀)-向量空间的维数有下界 √(s/log s)。该结果覆盖 |z₀|≤1 的一般周期 f，并在除去复平面上有限条割线外，对 |z₀|>1（级数不收敛、需解析延拓）的情形仍然成立，从而推广了 Fischler 关于 Li_i(z₀) 的结果。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个更一般的 Lerch 型级数取值的线性无关性下界，统一并推广了多重对数情形，显示积分表示与 Shidlovskii 引理结合可有效处理周期系数与解析延拓下的线性无关问题。

Abstract: For any non-zero periodic function f\,: Z $\rightarrow$ C of period N ___ 1 and any non-zero algebraic number z\_0 that doesn't lie on a half-line [e^2i${\ell}$$π$/N , e^2i${\ell}$$π$/N $\infty$[, 0 ___ ${\ell}$ ___ N -1, we give a lower bound of order sqrt{s/log(s)} on the dimension of the Q(z\_0 )-vector space spanned by the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 )\,: sum\_{m=1}^$\infty$ f(m)z\_0^m/m^i. This generalizes a result Fischler proved in 2026, corresponding to f identically equal to 1 and |z\_0| ___ 1: in this case, the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) = Li\_i (z\_0 ) are polylogarithm values. Except for a finite number of cuts in the complex plane, our result still holds when |z\_0 | > 1, that is in a domain where the series definition above for the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) doesn't converge anymore, and we make sense of these numbers through analytic continuation. To obtain this result, we construct linear combinations of the numbers L(f, i, z\_0 ) using a refined version of Siegel's lemma, and we apply to them a linear independence criterion generalizing the one used by Fischler. To check the assumptions of this criterion, we rely on an integral representation of the polylogarithm functions and on a ''Shidlovskii lemma''.

</details>


### [143] [Maximal volume of convex bodies avoiding random points and lacunary dilates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09790)
*Yuval Peres,Bohan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在模 $\mathbb Z^d$ 意义下，躲避前 $N$ 个 dilatates 的凸体的渐近最大体积，给出了精确估计，并构造了反例说明 sublacunary 尺度上稀疏性不能替代 lacunarity。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定在动力系统与几何测度论交叉背景下，躲避点集扩张结构的凸体最大体积的渐近行为，并厘清 lacunary 与 sublacunary 尺度条件的关键差别。

Method: 对正实 lacunary 序列及具有多项式 Fourier 衰减的概率测度 $\mu$，考察 $\mu$-几乎处处 $x$ 的模 $\mathbb Z^d$ 前 $N$ 个 dilatates；分别讨论固定凸体的 homothet 与单位立方体内所有凸体两类情形，并构造一维反例。

Result: 固定凸体的 homothet 情形最大体积渐近为 $(\log N)/N$；允许单位立方体内所有凸体时为 $d(\log N)/N$；对固定凸体 $\Omega\subset\mathbb R^d$ 内独立均匀点，空凸体最大体积几乎必然渐近为 $d\operatorname{vol}(\Omega)(\log N)/N$。

Conclusion: 刻画了躲避 dilatates 的凸体最大体积的精确渐近阶，并通过一维反例表明 lacunarity 条件不能被 sublacunary 尺度上的稀疏性替代。

Abstract: For a positive real lacunary sequence and any probability measure $μ$ with polynomial Fourier decay, we determine, for $μ$-almost every $x$, the asymptotic maximal volume of convex bodies avoiding the first $N$ dilates of $x$ modulo $\mathbb Z^d$. Among homothets of a fixed convex body, the maximal volume is asymptotic to $(\log N)/N$. When all convex bodies in the unit cube are allowed, it is asymptotic to $d(\log N)/N$. For independent uniform points in a fixed convex body $Ω\subset\mathbb R^d$, the maximal volume of an empty convex body is asymptotic to $d\operatorname{vol}(Ω)(\log N)/N$ almost surely. We also show, by constructing a one-dimensional counterexample, that lacunarity cannot be replaced by sparsity on sublacunary scales.

</details>


### [144] [Translation-invariant equations in $\mathbb{F}_p^n$ and groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09887)
*Katalin Gyarmati*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限群中在线性方程 aX+bY=cZ+dU（a+b=c+d）下的无解子集最大规模问题，推广了经典 Sidon 集概念。作者用 Tao 的 slice rank 方法在 F_p^n 中给出严格上界，并在 Z_m 中构造出对特定非对称方程无解且对数密度约 0.5283 的大集合，突破 0.5 障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Sidon 集研究的是集合中不含 X+Y=Z+W 非平凡解的最大规模问题。本文希望将这一框架推广到更一般的线性方程 aX+bY=cZ+dU（系数满足 a+b=c+d），从而统一并扩展对"无解集合"极值行为的理解，并探索经典的 0.5 密度上界是否普遍成立。

Method: 采用 Tao 的 slice rank（切片秩）方法证明上界；对 F_p^n 中的无解集合建立严格上界；对循环群 Z_m，通过显式构造方法给出对非对称方程 X+5Y=3U+3Z 无解的大集合，并分析其系数模 8 的规律以推广构造。

Result: 在 F_p^n 中得到了此类方程无解集合规模的严格上界；在 Z_m 中构造出对 X+5Y=3U+3Z 无解的集合，对数密度约为 0.5283，突破了 0.5；并将该高密度现象推广到系数满足模 8 简单规律的一族更广泛方程。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对一般线性方程而言 0.5 的密度障碍并非普遍成立，通过合适构造可达到更高密度；slice rank 方法对上界有效，而系数模 8 的规律刻画了高密度构造的适用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore how large a subset of a finite group can be without containing non-trivial solutions to linear equations of the form $aX+bY=cZ+dU$ (where $a+b=c+d$). These equations naturally generalize the classic concept of Sidon sets. Using Tao's slice rank method, we first establish strict upper bounds for the size of such solution-free sets in the vector space $\mathbb{F}_{p}^{n}$. Next, we turn to cyclic groups $\mathbb{Z}_{m}$ and construct surprisingly large sets that have no solutions for the asymmetric equation $X+5Y=3U+3Z$. These constructions achieve a logarithmic density of roughly $0.5283$, breaking the expected $0.5$ barrier for any sufficiently large modulus $m$. Finally, we show that this high-density behavior extends to a wider family of equations whose coefficients follow simple rules modulo $8$.

</details>


### [145] [Terminal Blocks of Primes in Pisot Numeration Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10026)
*Sungkon Chang,Johann Verwee*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了 Pisot 整数进制系统中固定末端字块的素数定理：当主导根为 Pisot 数且特征多项式为其极小多项式时，总位长为 m 的末端字块在素数中以渐近频率 φ^{-m} 出现。在 Zeckendorf 情形下解决了近期猜想。证明将末端条件转化为 Rauzy 柱窗，再转化为紧环面上的线性轨道；对伴随替换，所需的重数一 Rauzy 几何自动满足（反转替换词后 Barge 纯离散定理适用）。由 Rauzy 环面还得到替换不动词的素数定理（每个有限因子在素数起始位置以通常因子频率出现），证明了 Drmota–Müllner–Spiegelhofer 提出的 Tribonacci 素数定理。环面模型进一步给出多项式抽样律、与固定同余类的渐近独立性、固定移位相关公式及 Möbius 正交性，并结合已有素数定理给出 Chebotarev 的末端精化，以及任意固定末端素数类中包含公差为多对数界的任意长等差数列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将经典素数定理推广到具有自相似/替换结构的整数进制系统，刻画素数在特定数字约束（末端字块）下的分布规律；同时解决 Zeckendorf 情形下的公开猜想，并回应 Drmota–Müllner–Spiegelhofer 关于 Tribonacci 素数定理的提议。

Method: 方法上，将末端条件翻译为 Rauzy 柱窗（cylinder windows），进而转化为紧环面上的线性轨道；利用伴随替换（companion substitutions）的多重性一 Rauzy 几何，通过反转替换词使 Barge 纯离散性定理适用。Rauzy 环面模型还被用于推导抽样律、同余独立性、移位相关与 Möbius 正交性等。

Result: 主要结果：在 Pisot 整数进制系统中，末端块字数 m 的固定末端字块在素数中的渐近频率为 φ^{-m}；Zeckendorf 情形下的猜想得证。替换不动词中每个有限因子在素数起始位置以其通常因子频率出现，Tribonacci 素数定理成立。此外得到多项式抽样律、与固定同余类的渐近独立性、固定移位相关公式与 Möbius 正交性；结合已有素数定理给出 Chebotarev 的末端精化，并证明每个固定末端素数类含公差多对数有界的任意长等差数列。

Conclusion: 结论是：Pisot 进制系统中的素数在固定末端字块上具有与自相似几何一致的渐近频率，Rauzy 环面提供了统一框架，可同时处理末端约束与替换不动词，并导出多种算术与动力系统层面的分布性质。

Abstract: We prove a prime number theorem for fixed terminal block words in Pisot integer numeration systems. If the dominant root $\varphi$ is a Pisot number and the characteristic polynomial $P_h$ is its minimal polynomial, every terminal block word of total digit-length $m$ occurs among the primes with asymptotic frequency $\varphi^{-m}$. In the Zeckendorf case this resolves a recent conjecture. The proof converts terminal conditions into Rauzy cylinder windows and then into a linear orbit on a compact torus. For these companion substitutions, the required multiplicity-one Rauzy geometry is automatic: Barge's pure-discreteness theorem applies after reversal of the substitution words.
  The Rauzy torus also gives a prime number theorem for the substitution fixed word: every finite factor occurs at prime starting positions with its ordinary factor frequency. This proves the Tribonacci prime-number theorem suggested by Drmota--Müllner--Spiegelhofer. The toral model further yields polynomial sampling laws, asymptotic independence from fixed congruence classes, fixed-shift correlation formulas, and Möbius orthogonality. Combined with established prime theorems, it gives a terminal refinement of Chebotarev and shows that every fixed terminal prime class contains arbitrarily long arithmetic progressions with polylogarithmically bounded common difference.

</details>


### [146] [Spacing statistics for a point scatterer on the cubic three-torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10031)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究立方三环面上不动点相互作用的特征值间距分布，证明其统计规律可由三平方同余密度构造的随机亚纯函数刻画，并给出小间距分布的精确渐近形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画固定点相互作用在立方三环面上的新特征值统计规律，理解其相邻间距的极限分布及其与相互作用参数无关的普适性。

Method: 结合周期均方逼近、加权Hilbert变换估计，以及保留素数间相互作用的Fourier估计，将间距分布与随机亚纯函数的连续零点联系起来。

Result: 均值一相邻间距收敛到与相互作用参数无关的概率律，该律由三平方同余密度构造的随机亚纯函数连续零点刻画；小间距分布为 $s^5\Psi(\log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)$，其中 $\Psi$ 连续、正且一周期。

Conclusion: 建立了立方三环面上不动点相互作用特征值间距的极限概率律及其小间距渐近表达，揭示了由三平方同余密度与素数相互作用决定的普适统计结构。

Abstract: We study the new eigenvalues of a fixed point interaction on the cubic three-torus. Their mean-one consecutive spacings converge to a probability law independent of the interaction parameter. We characterize this law by the consecutive zeros of a random meromorphic function built from three-squares congruence densities. Its small-gap distribution has the form $s^5Ψ(\log_2(s^{-2}))+o(s^5)$, where $Ψ$ is continuous, positive, and one-periodic. The proof combines periodic mean-square approximation, a weighted Hilbert-transform estimate, and Fourier estimates that retain the interactions between primes.

</details>


### [147] [Consecutive Rankin-Cohen Bases, Full-Spark Periods, and Divisor-Tau Congruences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10052)
*Kelvin Lam*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Mellin周期泛函行列式的严格正性，给出了基本半区间上周期的满秩定理，并验证了Xue的奇偶混合周期独立性猜想。由此得到首类Rankin-Cohen括号构成S_K的基、确定Eisenstein括号行列式符号，并给出除数-tau卷积C_r(n)的统一精确全权公式、素数逐项约化及稀疏Ramanujan型特化刻画，最后将Cramer系统化为单变量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在证明Mellin周期泛函行列式的严格正性，从而解决周期独立性问题（包括Xue的混合奇偶周期独立性猜想），并为模形式空间S_K寻找自然的基与显式公式。

Method: 对Mellin周期泛函的行列式证明严格正性；利用基本半区间上的满秩（full-spark）定理；使用首类Rankin-Cohen括号[E_{2r+10-2j},E_{2j+2}]_1构造基；将坐标恒等式模素数进行约化；对同次f,g∈Q[E_4,E_6]证明括号恒等式[f,g]_1/Δ=-3456·det((f_{E_4},f_{E_6}),(g_{E_4},g_{E_6}))，将Cramer系统约化为T=E_6^2/E_4^3的单变量坐标问题。

Result: 证明了Mellin周期泛函行列式的严格正性与基本半区间上的满秩定理；验证了Xue的混合奇偶周期独立性猜想；得到连续的d个首类Rankin-Cohen括号构成S_K的基；确定了所有可行的有序Eisenstein括号行列式的符号；给出除数-tau卷积C_r(n)=Σ_{m=1}^{n-1}σ_{2r+1}(m)τ(n-m)的统一精确全权公式；得到每个权重下无穷多素数的标准逐项约化，并通过消失的Cramer坐标刻画稀疏Ramanujan型特化；证明[f,g]_1/Δ的显式行列式公式，将Cramer系统约化为单变量问题。

Conclusion: 论文通过Mellin周期泛函行列式的正性，统一解决了周期独立性、模形式空间S_K的基构造、Eisenstein括号行列式符号以及除数-tau卷积的精确全权公式，并将相关Cramer系统约化为E_6^2/E_4^3的单变量坐标问题，为素数约化与稀疏Ramanujan型特化提供了新框架。

Abstract: For $r\ge1$, let $K=2r+14$ and $d=\dim S_K$. We prove strict positivity for determinants of Mellin period functionals, yielding a full-spark theorem for periods in a fundamental half-range and, in particular, the mixed odd--even period independence conjecture of Xue. As a consequence, the consecutive first Rankin--Cohen brackets $[E_{2r+10-2j},E_{2j+2}]_1$, $1\le j\le d$, form a basis of $S_K$, and we determine the signs of all admissible ordered determinants of first Eisenstein brackets. This gives a uniform exact all-weight formula for the divisor--tau convolution $C_r(n)=\sum_{m=1}^{n-1}σ_{2r+1}(m)τ(n-m)$. Reducing the same coordinate identity modulo primes, we obtain canonical prime-wise reductions for infinitely many primes in every weight and characterize sparse Ramanujan-type specializations through vanishing Cramer coordinates. Finally, for homogeneous $f,g\in\mathbf Q[E_4,E_6]$, we prove $[f,g]_1/Δ=-3456\det((f_{E_4},f_{E_6}),(g_{E_4},g_{E_6}))$, reducing the Cramer system to a one-variable coordinate problem in $T=E_6^2/E_4^3$.

</details>


### [148] [Invariant Primes in Lubin-Tate Space and Hovey-Strickland at Every Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10072)
*Chenglong Ma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对任意高度 n 与素数 p，A_n = R_n/(p) 中在 Morava 稳定子群某个开子群下稳定的素理想恰为高度理想 (u_1,…,u_j)，并证明 R_n 中避开 p 的稳定素理想为零；由此推出 Hovey–Strickland 关于可对偶 K(n)-局部谱范畴中厚张量理想的分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是刻画 A_n 与 R_n 中在 Morava 稳定子群开子群作用下稳定的素理想，从而获得 radical-ideal 分类，并借此推出可对偶 K(n)-局部谱范畴中厚张量理想的 Hovey–Strickland 分类。核心困难在于特殊纤维上稳定素理想的分类，需要新的几何与形变论工具。

Method: 特殊纤维论证是局部且几何的：先用单参数曲线切割不变素理想，由 Cartier 结构方程构造光滑形式商 Q 与可区分子群 H；将 H 的一般纤维等同于连通-étale 扩张的形变空间。用 Cartier 障碍映射（来自完整 Honda 自同态序）与通过完备化万有覆盖对 Tate 向量的求值进行比较；用 Fargues–Fontaine 向量丛给出周期检测陈述；再用 Chai 刚性定理在重正化赋值后把同态检测提升为形式 Zariski 稠密性；最后用一致固定-jet 论证把稠密性转移到 Morava 稳定子轨道。一般纤维断言则借助 Gross–Hopkins 周期映射单独证明。

Result: 证明了：(1) 对每个高度 n 和素数 p，A_n 中在 Morava 稳定子群某开子群下稳定的素理想恰为高度理想 (u_1,…,u_j)；(2) R_n 中避开 p 的稳定素理想为零。所得 radical-ideal 分类通过 Barthel–Heard–Naumann 的正向蕴含，推出可对偶 K(n)-局部谱范畴中厚张量理想的 Hovey–Strickland 分类。

Conclusion: 论文完成了稳定素理想的分类，并以此统一导出厚张量理想的 Hovey–Strickland 分类；其方法结合 Cartier 理论、Fargues–Fontaine 向量丛、Chai 刚性定理与 Gross–Hopkins 周期映射，为 chromatic 同伦论中的分类问题提供了几何化路径。

Abstract: Let $H_n$ be the one-dimensional Honda formal group of height $n$ over $\mathbf F_{p^n}$ and let \[ R_n=W(\mathbf F_{p^n})[[u_1,\ldots,u_{n-1}]],\qquad A_n=R_n/(p). \] We prove, for every height $n$ and every prime $p$, that the prime ideals of $A_n$ stable under an open subgroup of the Morava stabilizer group are exactly the height ideals $(u_1,\ldots,u_j)$. We also prove that a stable prime of $R_n$ avoiding $p$ is zero. The resulting radical-ideal classification implies the Hovey-Strickland classification of thick tensor ideals in the category of dualizable $K(n)$-local spectra via the forward implication of Barthel-Heard-Naumann.
  The special-fiber argument is local and geometric. After cutting an invariant prime by a one-parameter curve, we construct from the Cartier structure equation a smooth formal quotient $\mathcal{Q}$ and a distinguished subgroup $\mathcal{H}$. The generic fiber of $\mathcal{H}$ is identified with the deformation space of connected-étale extensions. A Cartier obstruction map from the full Honda endomorphism order is compared with evaluation on Tate vectors through completed universal covers. Fargues-Fontaine vector bundles give a period-detection statement. Chai's rigidity theorem then promotes detection by homomorphisms to formal Zariski density after a renormalization of the valuation. A uniform fixed-jet argument transfers this density to Morava-stabilizer orbits. The generic-fiber assertion is proved separately from the Gross-Hopkins period map.

</details>


### [149] [Generating sets for maximal orders in rational quaternion algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10139)
*Kirsten Eisenträger,Eyal Z. Goren,Annamaria Iezzi,Harun Kir,Eda Kırımlı,Jonathan R. Love,William E. Mahaney,Jennifer Park,Maria Sabitova*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 考虑有理正定四元数代数中极大序 $\mathfrak{O}$，对与判别式互素的素数 $\ell$，研究由范数为 $\ell$ 的幂且整体生成 $\mathfrak{O}$（作为 $\mathbb{Z}$-代数）的元素构成的子集，证明其存在性并讨论对超奇异同源图的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究四元数序中范数为 $\ell$-幂的元素如何生成序本身，可为超奇异同源图等算术与计算数论问题提供结构性与显式工具。

Method: 证明两个生成性定理：对足够大的 $k$（给出显式界）$\mathfrak{O}$ 可由范数 $\ell^k$ 的元素生成；并构造至多三个范数为 $\ell$ 的幂的元素生成 $\mathfrak{O}$。作为中间步骤，对未必极大的四元数序 $\mathcal{O}$，给出 Brandt 矩阵代数结构结果及 theta 函数系数的显式界；并进行计算实验。

Result: 证明了：对任意大于显式界的 $k$，$\mathfrak{O}$ 可由范数 $\ell^k$ 的元素生成；且 $\mathfrak{O}$ 存在至多三个元素、每个范数为 $\ell$ 的幂的生成集。并建立 Brandt 矩阵代数的结构结果与 theta 函数系数的显式界。

Conclusion: 这些生成性结果与显式界为四元数序的代数生成问题提供定量结论，并对超奇异同源图的研究具有意义。

Abstract: Given a maximal order $\mathfrak{O}$ in a rational definite quaternion algebra, and a prime $\ell$ coprime to the discriminant of $\mathfrak{O}$, this paper considers subsets of $\mathfrak{O}$ consisting of elements with $\ell$-power norms that together generate $\mathfrak{O}$ as a $\mathbb{Z}$-algebra. We prove two theorems about the existence of such sets: the first states that $\mathfrak{O}$ is generated by elements of norm $\ell^k$ for any $k$ larger than an explicit bound, and the second states that there is a generating set for $\mathfrak{O}$ consisting of at most three elements, each with norm a power of $\ell$. We discuss implications for the study of supersingular isogeny graphs. As steps towards these theorems, for quaternion orders $\mathcal{O}$ that are not necessarily maximal, we also prove structural results about the algebra of Brandt matrices for $\mathcal{O}$ and explicit bounds on the coefficients of the theta function of $\mathcal{O}$. Computational experiments are also discussed.

</details>


### [150] [Improved average and almost-all bounds for $G(n)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10177)
*Chiara Bellotti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究与单位群互素的整数生成子最小上界函数 $G(n)$，改进了其求和估计并证明了几乎所有 $n$ 的无条件对数阶上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $G(n)$ 刻画了 $(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)^\times$ 中需要多大范围的、与 $n$ 互素的整数即可生成整个单位群，是数论中原始根与生成元问题的自然定量推广；此前已知的求和高阶估计 $\ll x(\log x)^{97}$ 过于粗糙，且点wise 最优阶 $\leq(\log n)^2$ 仅在 GRH 下成立，因此作者希望削弱假设并显著改进平均阶。

Method: 通过解析数论方法（涉及单位群结构、乘法特征与筛法/分布估计等技术）对满足小范围内互素整数生成 $(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)^\times$ 的例外 $n$ 进行计数与求和估计，从而同时得到求和上界与几乎处处的点wise 上界。

Result: 证明了：对任意 $\varepsilon>0$，$\sum_{n\leq x}G(n)\ll_\varepsilon x(\log x)^{8/3+\varepsilon}$，将先前的指数 $97$ 改进为 $8/3+\varepsilon$；并证明了无条件地几乎所有 $n$ 满足 $G(n)\leq(\log n)^2$。

Conclusion: 作者显著改进了 $G(n)$ 的平均阶，并在无条件下获得了几乎所有 $n$ 的对数指数为 $2$ 的点wise 上界，与 GRH 下的经典点wise 结果达到相同的对数阶。

Abstract: In this paper we study the least positive integer $G(n)$ such that the integers $a\leq G(n)$ with $(a,n)=1$ generate $(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)^\times$. We prove that, for every $\varepsilon>0$, \[
  \sum_{n\leq x}G(n)\ll_\varepsilon x(\log x)^{8/3+\varepsilon}, \] improving the previously known bound $\ll x(\log x)^{97}$. We also prove that $G(n)\leq(\log n)^2$ for almost all $n$, unconditionally. Thus, for almost all $n$, we obtain the same logarithmic exponent $2$ as in the classical pointwise bound under GRH.

</details>


### [151] [Irrationality exponents of logarithms of positive rational numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10192)
*Jingwen Liu,Kai Jiang,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出分离权重行列式论证，证明对每个正有理数 r≠1 有 μ(log r)=2，并给出该论证的全部参数选择与解析界；附录补充几何推广与显式有限次数界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为 μ(log r)=2（r 为正有理数、r≠1）提供一个直接的"分离权重行列式"证明，避免依赖间接方法；同时把该论证的细节（参数、分母估计、行平移、解析行列式界）完整固定下来，以便复核。

Method: 核心工具是"分离权重行列式论证"：先证明所需的移动中心插值定理（moving-centre interpolation theorem），再对任意固定的 r=a/b 给出该论证的全部参数选择、分母估计、行平移以及解析行列式上界，从而得到 μ(log r)=2；有理线性组合与有理仿射变换作为推论得到。

Result: 对每个正有理数 r≠1，得到 μ(log r)=2；并给出该论证的完整参数、分母估计、行平移与解析行列式界。

Conclusion: 论文将分离权重行列式论证系统化并向外推广（有理线性组合、有理仿射变换、几何推广及显式有限次数界）。作者声明本文仍为受审核的研究草稿，重新组织不等于对其数学结论的独立认证。

Abstract: We present a direct separated-weight determinant argument for μ(log r) = 2 for every positive rational number r = 1. We first prove the moving-centre interpolation theorem used in the argument. We then fix an arbitrary r = a/b and give all parameter choices, denominator estimates, row translations, and analytic determinant bounds for that same argument. Rational linear combinations and rational affine changes follow as corollaries.Supplementary geometric extensions and an explicit finite-degree bound are collected in the appendices. This remains a research draft under audit; the reorganization does not constitute independent certification of its mathematical conclusions.

</details>


### [152] [Intersecting a curve in an abelian variety with multiples of another curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10212)
*Fabrizio Barroero,Gabriel A. Dill,Lars Kuehne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解答了Levin问题在阿贝尔簇上的类比，证明了Zilber–Pink猜想在该情形下的预测，关键技术是加强了Vojta与Rémond的高度不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Levin提出：对于$\mathbb{G}_m^n$（$n\geq 3$）中两条给定曲线，一条曲线上的点若其某个非零整数倍落在另一条曲线上，这些点构成的轨迹如何描述？作者研究该问题的阿贝尔簇类比，并试图证实Zilber–Pink猜想在此情形下的预测。

Method: 核心工具是对Vojta和Rémond的高度不等式进行加强，并据此处理阿贝尔簇上曲线间非零整数倍关系的点集轨迹问题。

Result: 证明了Levin问题的阿贝尔类比，所得结论与Zilber–Pink猜想在该情形的预测一致；同时给出了Vojta–Rémond高度不等式的强化版本。

Conclusion: 本文确立了Levin问题阿贝尔类比的确切答案，验证了Zilber–Pink猜想的相关预测，并表明加强的高度不等式是解决此类问题的重要工具。

Abstract: Levin asked what can be said about the locus of points lying on a given curve in $\mathbb{G}_m^n$ that have a non-zero integer multiple on another given curve in $\mathbb{G}_m^n$ for $n \geq 3$. We give a definite answer to the abelian analogue of Levin's question, proving what is predicted by the Zilber--Pink conjecture in this case. An important ingredient in our proof is a strengthening of a height inequality by Vojta and Rémond.

</details>


### [153] [Local Factors in the BSD Conjecture: A Unified Statistical View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10243)
*David Kurniadi Angdinata,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono,Ashvin Swaminathan,Sameera Vemulapalli*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为短Weierstrass形式的椭圆曲线$E/\mathbb{Q}$构造多变量Euler乘积生成函数，编码Tamagawa积$Tam(E)=\prod_p c_p(E)$，并据此计算Tamagawa数的极限分布、协方差、矩与尾界，得到超过半数曲线Tamagawa积平凡等定量比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tamagawa数$Tam(E)=\prod_p c_p(E)$是椭圆曲线算术的重要不变量，但对其整体分布规律（如取平凡值的比例）缺乏系统的解析工具。作者希望借助生成函数方法获得Tamagawa积的精确分布与统计性质。

Method: 利用Tate算法导出按局部约化数据（约化类型等）索引的局部因子，构造关于局部约化数据的Euler乘积，再通过专门化得到多变量Euler乘积生成函数来编码Tamagawa积；基于该生成函数计算四个关键统计量的极限分布、精确协方差以及矩界和尾界。

Result: 给出了Tamagawa积在高度排序下的极限分布等统计结果：超过半数曲线Tamagawa积为平凡（所有$c_p=1$）；约42.2%恰有一个素数处局部Tamagawa数非平凡；仅约6.8%恰有两个这样的素数。并给出相应的精确协方差及矩和尾界。

Conclusion: 多变量Euler乘积生成函数为研究Tamagawa数的整体分布提供了有效框架，得到具体而精确的比例与统计界限；论文结果已由AxiomProver在Lean中自动形式化。

Abstract: For elliptic curves $E/\mathbb{Q}$ in short Weierstrass form \[ E=E(a_4,a_6): y^2=x^3+a_4x+a_6, \] we derive a multivariable Euler product generating function which encodes the Tamagawa product $Tam(E)=\prod_p c_p(E)$. Using this generating function, we compute limiting distributions, exact covariances, and moment and tail bounds for four important statistics on the Tamagawa number; for example we show that more than half of all curves in this height ordering have trivial Tamagawa product, about $42.2\%$ have exactly one prime with nontrivial local Tamagawa number, and only about $6.8\%$ have exactly two such primes.
